st d=read(),key=sub+'/'+current.id,p=d[key]||{};p.misses=(p.misses||0)+1;d[key]=p;save(d)}
function markHelp(){gotHelp=true;run.help=true;const d=read(),key=sub+'/'+current.id,p=d[key]||{};p.hints=(p.hints||0)+1;d[key]=p;save(d)}
function nextStep(){step++;attempted=false;gotWrong=false;gotHelp=false;chosenOrigin='';if(step>=current.steps.length){end();return}render()}
function end(){finished=true;const d=read(),key=sub+'/'+current.id,p=d[key]||{};const t=now();const prior=p.lastCompleted||0;const wasClean=!run.help&&!run.wrong;const due=prior&&t-prior>=12*HOUR;
 p.attempts=(p.attempts||0)+1;if(wasClean){if(!prior){p.clean=(p.clean||0)+1;p.firstClean=t}else if(due){p.reviewed=(p.reviewed||0)+1;p.clean=(p.clean||0)+1;p.lastCleanReview=t}}
 p.lastCompleted=t;p.nextReview=t+12*HOUR;p.lastWasClean=wasClean;p.lastHelp=run.help;p.lastWrong=run.wrong;d[key]=p;save(d);render()}
function record(kind,v){run.answers.push({step,kind,value:v,helped:gotHelp||gotWrong});const s=current.steps[step];$('v89Feedback').innerHTML=`<strong>${kind==='correct'?'That’s it.':'Here’s how.'}</strong><p>${esc(s.why)}</p>${!gotHelp&&!gotWrong?'<small>Worked out without a clue.</small>':'<small>This step counts as practice with help.</small>'}<div class="v89-follow"><button type="button" id="v89Next">${step===2?'Finish this problem':'Next step'}</button></div>`;$('v89Next').onclick=nextStep;disableStepInput();}
function disableStepInput(){const host=$('v89Problem');host.querySelectorAll('[data-v89-choice],#v89Answer,#v89Check,[data-v89-square],#v89Hint,#v89Show').forEach(b=>b.disabled=true)}
function submitNumber(){const s=current.steps[step],box=$('v89Answer'),raw=box.value.trim().replace(/,/g,'');if(!raw){$('v89Feedback').textContent='Enter a number before checking.';return}const match=raw.match(/^([+-]?(?:\d+(?:\.\d*)?|\.\d+))(?:\s*(?:m\/s²|m\/s|kg·m\/s|kWh|joules?|j|watts?|w|pascals?|pa|metres?|meters?|m|seconds?|s|amperes?|a|newtons?|n|°c|cells?|m²))?$/i);if(!match){$('v89Feedback').textContent='Enter the number alone, or the number with the unit shown in the question.';return}const value=Number(match[1]);const tolerance=Math.max(0.000001,Math.abs(s.answer)*0.000001);if(Math.abs(value-s.answer)<=tolerance){record('correct',value);return}markWrong();const custom=s.wrong[String(value)];$('v89Feedback').innerHTML=`<strong>Not quite.</strong><p>${esc(custom||'Check the quantities and units. Try working it out one step at a time.')}</p><small>You can try again or ask for a clue.</small>`;box.focus()}
function submitChoice(i){const s=current.steps[step];if(i===s.answer){record('correct',i);return}markWrong();$('v89Feedback').innerHTML=`<strong>Not this one.</strong><p>${esc(s.wrong[i])}</p><small>Try another choice or ask for a clue.</small>`;document.querySelectorAll('[data-v89-choice]').forEach(b=>{if(+b.dataset.v89Choice===i){b.classList.add('v89-wrong');b.disabled=true}})}
function submitSquare(square){const s=current.steps[step];if(!chosenOrigin){if(!currentBoard[square]||currentBoard[square]!==currentBoard[square].toUpperCase()){const p=currentBoard[square];$('v89Feedback').textContent=p?'Select a white piece to move.':'Select the white piece you want to move.';return}chosenOrigin=square;renderBoard();$('v89Feedback').textContent='Now choose where that piece goes.';return}
 if(square===chosenOrigin){chosenOrigin='';renderBoard();$('v89Feedback').textContent='Choose a piece to move.';return}
 if(chosenOrigin===s.from&&square===s.to){const piece=currentBoard[s.from];delete currentBoard[s.from];currentBoard[s.to]=(piece==='P'&&square.endsWith('8'))?'Q':piece;chosenOrigin='';renderBoard();record('correct',s.from+'-'+s.to);return}
 markWrong();const wrongPiece=chosenOrigin!==s.from;chosenOrigin='';renderBoard();$('v89Feedback').innerHTML=`<strong>Try a different move.</strong><p>${wrongPiece?'Look for the white piece described in the question.':'That destination does not achieve the goal. Check the movement pattern and what the move attacks.'}</p><small>Choose your starting square again.</small>`}
function hint(show){if(!gotHelp)markHelp();const s=current.steps[step];$('v89Feedback').innerHTML=show?`<strong>How to work it out</strong><p>${esc(s.why)}</p><p class="v89-answerline">${esc(s.type==='number'?'Answer: '+s.answer:s.type==='choice'?'Answer: '+s.choices[s.answer]:'Move: '+s.from+' to '+s.to)}</p><div class="v89-follow"><button id="v89Skip" type="button">Continue with help</button></div>`:`<strong>A clue</strong><p>${esc(s.hint)}</p><small>Have another go when you’re ready.</small>`;if(show){$('v89Skip').onclick=()=>{run.answers.push({step,kind:'revealed'});nextStep()}}}
function graph(){const kind=current.picture; if(!kind)return ''; const x0=45,y0=185,w=320,h=160;let curves='';let label='';let plot=[];
 if(kind==='line'){label='The line through (1, 3) and (3, 7)';for(let x=0;x<=5;x+=.08)plot.push([x,2*x+1])}
 else if(kind==='triangle'){label='Velocity rises from 0 to 12 m/s in 4 s';for(let x=0;x<=4;x+=.08)plot.push([x,3*x])}
 else if(kind==='parabola'){label='Position s(t) = t² + 2t';for(let x=0;x<=4;x+=.08)plot.push([x,x*x+2*x])}
 else if(kind==='curve'){label='The curve y = x² − 4x + 1 turns at x = 2';for(let x=0;x<=4;x+=.08)plot.push([x,x*x-4*x+1])}
 if(!plot.length)return '';const yMin=Math.min(0,...plot.map(v=>v[1])),yMax=Math.max(1,...plot.map(v=>v[1]));const xMax=Math.max(...plot.map(v=>v[0]));const px=(x)=>x0+x/xMax*w,py=y=>y0-(y-yMin)/(yMax-yMin)*h;if(kind==='line')curves=[[1,3],[3,7]].map(([a,b])=>`<circle cx="${px(a)}" cy="${py(b)}" r="5" fill="#b36e28"/>`).join('');if(kind==='triangle')curves=`<path d="M${px(0)} ${py(0)} L${px(4)} ${py(12)} L${px(4)} ${py(0)} Z" fill="#d7e8ed" opacity=".75"/>`;const path=plot.map((p,i)=>(i?'L':'M')+px(p[0]).toFixed(1)+' '+py(p[1]).toFixed(1)).join(' ');return `<figure class="v89-figure"><svg viewBox="0 0 410 220" role="img" aria-label="${esc(label)}"><path d="M45 26 V185 H380" stroke="#97b4c2" stroke-width="2" fill="none"/><line x1="45" y1="${py(0)}" x2="380" y2="${py(0)}" stroke="#b7ccd4" stroke-dasharray="4 4"/><path d="${path}" stroke="#226b8a" stroke-width="3.5" fill="none" stroke-linecap="round"/>${curves}<text x="382" y="205" fill="#557487" text-anchor="end" font-size="12">x / time →</text><text x="5" y="17" fill="#557487" font-size="12">y ↑</text></svg><figcaption>${esc(label)}</figcaption></figure>`}
function forcePicture(){if(current.picture!=='forces')return '';return `<figure class="v89-figure"><svg viewBox="0 0 410 165" role="img" aria-label="A cart with a 12 newton force to the right and 4 newton friction to the left"><path d="M25 128 H385" stroke="#abc2cc" stroke-width="3"/><rect x="165" y="57" width="91" height="62" rx="9" fill="#dae9ef" stroke="#317491" stroke-width="2"/><circle cx="183" cy="122" r="9" fill="#597a8b"/><circle cx="239" cy="122" r="9" fill="#597a8b"/><text x="210" y="92" text-anchor="middle" font-size="19" font-weight="700" fill="#22536a">4 kg</text><path d="M260 78 H381 M381 78 l-15 -9 M381 78 l-15 9" stroke="#237852" stroke-width="5" fill="none" stroke-linecap="round"/><text x="310" y="57" font-size="13" font-weight="700" fill="#237852">12 N</text><path d="M162 98 H51 M51 98 l15 -9 M51 98 l15 9" stroke="#bd7446" stroke-width="5" fill="none" stroke-linecap="round"/><text x="71" y="84" font-size="13" font-weight="700" fill="#9c5530">4 N</text></svg><figcaption>Opposing forces on a 4 kg cart</figcaption></figure>`}
function renderBoard(){const mount=$('v89Chessboard');if(!mount)return;let html='<div class="v89-board" role="group" aria-label="Chessboard, white at the bottom">';for(let rank=8;rank>=1;rank--)for(let fi=0;fi<8;fi++){const sq='abcdefgh'[fi]+rank,p=currentBoard[sq],light=((rank+fi)%2===0);html+=`<button type="button" data-v89-square="${sq}" class="v89-cell ${light?'v89-light':'v89-dark'} ${chosenOrigin===sq?'v89-picked':''}" aria-label="${sq}: ${p?pieceName[p]:'empty'}" title="${sq}" ${current.steps[step].type!=='move'?'disabled':''}><span class="v89-piece ${p&&p===p.toUpperCase()?'v89-white-piece':'v89-black-piece'}">${p?pieceGlyph[p]:''}</span><small class="v89-rank">${fi===0?rank:''}</small><small class="v89-file">${rank===1?'abcdefgh'[fi]:''}</small></button>`}html+='</div>';mount.innerHTML=html;mount.querySelectorAll('[data-v89-square]').forEach(b=>b.onclick=()=>submitSquare(b.dataset.v89Square))}
function renderChoices(s){const order=s.choices.map((_,i)=>i); // Shuffle order reproducibly per stage without altering answer identities.
 const seed=(current.id.length+step*31+Math.floor(run.startedAt/86400000))%order.length;order.push(...order.splice(0,seed));return `<div class="v89-choices">${order.map(i=>`<button data-v89-choice="${i}" type="button">${esc(s.choices[i])}</button>`).join('')}</div>`}
function lessonCards(){const d=read();return `<div class="v89-subjects">${SUBJECTS.map(s=>`<button class="${sub===s.id?'active':''}" type="button" data-v89-sub="${s.id}" aria-pressed="${sub===s.id}">${s.label}</button>`).join('')}</div><p class="v89-intro">Choose a problem. Each one has three short steps. Ask for a clue whenever you need one.</p><div class="v89-lessons">${subject(sub).items.map(x=>{const p=d[sub+'/'+x.id]||{};const due=!!p.lastCompleted&&now()-p.lastCompleted>=12*HOUR;const status=p.reviewed?'Reviewed from memory':due?'Ready to revisit':p.lastCompleted?'Practised':'New';return `<button type="button" class="v89-lesson" data-v89-lesson="${x.id}"><span><small>${esc(x.level)} · ${esc(status)}</small><strong>${esc(x.title)}</strong></span><span aria-hidden="true">›</span></button>`}).join('')}</div>`}
function finishView(){const p=progress(current);const clean=!run.wrong&&!run.help;return `<div class="v89-success"><span class="v89-kicker">Finished</span><h2>${esc(current.title)}</h2><p>${clean?'You worked through all three steps without hints or corrections.':'You finished the problem. Read back over any steps that needed a clue or correction.'}</p><p class="v89-status">${p.reviewed?'Clean later reviews: '+p.reviewed:p.nextReview?'Come back after a break to check what you remember.':'You can revisit this problem later.'}</p><div class="v89-actions"><button id="v89Again">Try again</button><button id="v89All">Other problems</button></div></div>`}
function render(){if(!overlay)return;const main=$('v89Main');$('v89Title').textContent=current?'Practice the next step':'Make the next move';if(!current){main.innerHTML=lessonCards();main.querySelectorAll('[data-v89-sub]').forEach(b=>b.onclick=()=>{sub=b.dataset.v89Sub;render()});main.querySelectorAll('[data-v89-lesson]').forEach(b=>b.onclick=()=>start(subject(sub).items.find(x=>x.id===b.dataset.v89Lesson)));return}
 if(finished){main.innerHTML=finishView();$('v89Again').onclick=()=>start(current);$('v89All').onclick=()=>{current=null;render()};return}
 const s=current.steps[step];main.innerHTML=`<div class="v89-top"><button id="v89Back" type="button">All problems</button><span>${esc(subject(sub).label)} · Step ${step+1} of 3</span></div><span class="v89-kicker">${esc(current.level)}</span><h2>${esc(current.title)}</h2><p class="v89-story">${esc(current.intro)}</p>${graph()||forcePicture()}${current.board?'<div class="v89-chess-layout"><div id="v89Chessboard"></div><p class="v89-boardtip">White moves up the board. Tap a white piece, then its destination.</p></div>':''}<div id="v89Problem"><h3>${esc(s.q)}</h3>${s.type==='choice'?renderChoices(s):s.type==='number'?`<form id="v89Form"><label for="v89Answer">Your answer</label><div class="v89-inputrow"><input id="v89Answer" autocomplete="off" inputmode="decimal" type="text" placeholder="Type a number" aria-label="Your numerical answer"><button id="v89Check" type="submit">Check</button></div></form>`:'<p class="v89-move-prompt">Choose the move on the board above.</p>'}<div class="v89-help"><button id="v89Hint" type="button">A clue</button><button id="v89Show" type="button">Show how to work it out</button></div><div id="v89Feedback" class="v89-feedback" role="status" aria-live="polite"></div></div>`;
 $('v89Back').onclick=()=>{current=null;render()};$('v89Hint').onclick=()=>hint(false);$('v89Show').onclick=()=>hint(true);
 if(s.type==='choice')main.querySelectorAll('[data-v89-choice]').forEach(b=>b.onclick=()=>submitChoice(+b.dataset.v89Choice));if(s.type==='number')$('v89Form').onsubmit=e=>{e.preventDefault();submitNumber()};if(current.board)renderBoard()}
function close(){if(overlay){overlay.classList.remove('open');document.body.classList.remove('v89-open');const l=$('v89Launch');if(l)l.focus()}}
function open(section){if(!overlay)mount();sub=SUBJECTS.some(s=>s.id===section)?section:sub;current=null;overlay.classList.add('open');document.body.classList.add('v89-open');render();$('v89Close').focus()}
function addLauncher(){const parent=document.querySelector('.v65-more-actions');if(parent&&!$('v89Launch')){const b=document.createElement('button');b.type='button';b.id='v89Launch';b.textContent='Solve a problem: calculus, physics and chess';b.onclick=()=>open();parent.append(b)}}
function mount(){if(!overlay){overlay=document.createElement('div');overlay.id='v89Overlay';overlay.innerHTML='<section id="v89Room" role="dialog" aria-modal="true" aria-label="Guided practice"><header><div><small>STUDY TABLE</small><strong id="v89Title">Make the next move</strong></div><button type="button" id="v89Close" aria-label="Close guided practice">×</button></header><main id="v89Main"></main></section>';document.body.append(overlay);$('v89Close').onclick=close;overlay.addEventListener('click',e=>{if(e.target===overlay)close()});document.addEventListener('keydown',e=>{if(e.key==='Escape'&&overlay.classList.contains('open'))close();if(e.key==='Tab'&&overlay.classList.contains('open')){const links=[...$('v89Room').querySelectorAll('button:not([disabled]),input:not([disabled])')].filter(b=>b.getClientRects().length);if(!links.length)return;if(e.shiftKey&&document.activeElement===links[0]){e.preventDefault();links[links.length-1].focus()}else if(!e.shiftKey&&document.activeElement===links[links.length-1]){e.preventDefault();links[0].focus()}}})}
 addLauncher();const parent=document.querySelector('#v35Portal')||document.body;const observer=new MutationObserver(addLauncher);observer.observe(parent,{childList:true,subtree:true})}
window.studyTableV89={open,close,subjects:SUBJECTS.map(s=>({id:s.id,count:s.items.length})),inspect:()=>({subject:sub,id:current?.id,step,finished,help:run.help,wrong:run.wrong,progress:read()})};
if(document.readyState==='loading')document.addEventListener('DOMContentLoaded',mount,{once:true});else mount();
})();

</script>
<script id="v90-foundation-script">

/* Study Table V90 — a calmer home and one view of progress. Existing stores remain authoritative. */
(()=>{'use strict';
const $=id=>document.getElementById(id);
const esc=s=>String(s??'').replace(/[&<>"']/g,c=>({'&':'&amp;','<':'&lt;','>':'&gt;','"':'&quot;','\'':'&#39;'}[c]));
const safe=k=>{try{const p=JSON.parse(localStorage.getItem(k)||'{}');return p&&typeof p==='object'&&!Array.isArray(p)?p:{}}catch{return {}}};
const names={piano:'Piano',spanish:'Spanish',chess:'Chess',calculus:'Calculus',physics:'Physics',medicine:'Medicine',history:'History',economics:'Economics',books:'Books',life:'Life'};
const KEY='studytable_v90_preferences';
const getPrefs=()=>safe(KEY);
function pref(patch){try{localStorage.setItem(KEY,JSON.stringify({...getPrefs(),...patch}))}catch{}}
function progressSnapshot(){
 const groups=Object.fromEntries(Object.keys(names).map(k=>[k,{name:names[k],lessons:0,practiced:0,independent:0,reviews:0,due:0}]));
 const now=Date.now();
 for(const [k,r] of Object.entries(safe('studytable_v35_progress'))){const subject=k.split(':')[0].toLowerCase();if(!groups[subject]||!r?.seen)continue;groups[subject].lessons++;if(r.mastered)groups[subject].independent++;}
 for(const [k,r] of Object.entries(safe('studytable_v89_guided'))){const subject=k.split('/')[0];if(!groups[subject]||!r?.attempts)continue;groups[subject].practiced++;if(r.clean)groups[subject].independent++;if(r.reviewed)groups[subject].reviews+=Math.min(99,Number(r.reviewed)||0);if(r.nextReview && r.nextReview<=now)groups[subject].due++;}
 const studio=safe('studytable_v86_practice');
 for(const [key,subject] of [['spanish','spanish'],['past','spanish'],['melodies','piano'],['ear','piano'],['ears','piano'],['piano','piano']]){
  const data=studio[key];if(!data||typeof data!=='object'||Array.isArray(data))continue;
  for(const r of Object.values(data)){
   if(!r||typeof r!=='object'||(!r.attempted&&!r.attempts&&!r.last))continue;
   groups[subject].practiced++;
   if(r.clean===true||r.independent===true||r.bestClean===true)groups[subject].independent++;
  }
 }
 const cases=safe('studytable_v83_cases');
 const caseRows=cases.records&&typeof cases.records==='object'?cases.records:{};
 for(const [k,r] of Object.entries(caseRows)){
  if(!r||typeof r!=='object'||!r.runs)continue;
  const subject=k.startsWith('hist-')?'history':k.startsWith('med-')?'medicine':null;
  if(!subject)continue;groups[subject].practiced++;
  if(r.clean)groups[subject].independent++;
  if(r.later)groups[subject].reviews+=Math.min(99,Number(r.later)||0);
  if(r.due&&r.due<=now)groups[subject].due++;
 }
 return {groups,sections:Object.keys(groups).length,active:Object.values(groups).filter(r=>r.lessons||r.practiced),at:now};
}
function more(){document.body.classList.toggle('v90-explore');const showing=document.body.classList.contains('v90-explore');pref({showMore:showing});const b=$('v90More');if(b){b.textContent=showing?'Hide extra choices':'More ways to learn';b.setAttribute('aria-expanded',String(showing))}return showing}
function subjectOpen(s){if(window.studyTableFullCourses?.courses.some(c=>c.subject===s)){window.studyTableFullCourses.open(s);return}if(window.studyTableV97&&window.studyTableV97.lessons.some(l=>l.subject===s)){window.studyTableV97.open(s);return}pref({lastSubject:s});document.body.classList.remove('v90-explore');if(['calculus','physics','chess'].includes(s)&&window.studyTableV89){window.studyTableV89.open(s);return}if(['spanish','piano'].includes(s)&&window.studyTableV88){window.studyTableV88.open(s);return}if(typeof v35Open==='function'&&['medicine','economics','books'].includes(s)){v35Open(s,0);return}more();}
function continueLearning(){
 if(window.studyTableV108){window.studyTableV108.continueTeaching();return}
 const p=getPrefs();const section=p.lastSubject;
 if(section&&names[section]){subjectOpen(section);return}
 const resume=$('resumeBtn');if(resume&&!resume.disabled){resume.click();return}
 if(typeof v35Open==='function'){v35Open('spanish',0);return}
 $('v21PathBtn')?.click();
}
function summarize(){
 const d=progressSnapshot();const used=d.active.length;
 const r=d.active.reduce((a,v)=>a+v.due,0);
 return used?`${used} subjects with saved activity${r?' · '+r+' problems ready to revisit':''}.`:'No saved practice yet. Pick one idea and give it a try.';
}
function dashboard(){const v=progressSnapshot(),rows=Object.values(v.groups).filter(x=>x.lessons||x.practiced);
 const root=$('v90Dashboard');if(!root)return;
 root.innerHTML=`<p class="v90-muted">Your lessons and practice are shown together here. These are activity counts, not a grade or a percentage of a subject mastered.</p>${rows.length?`<div class="v90-metrics">${rows.map(x=>`<section class="v90-metric"><h3>${esc(x.name)}</h3><p>${x.lessons} lessons visited · ${x.practiced} practice problems attempted</p><p>${x.independent} recorded independent successes${x.due?' · <strong>'+x.due+' ready to revisit</strong>':''}</p><button type="button" data-v90-sub="${x.name.toLowerCase()}">Practice ${esc(x.name)}</button></section>`).join('')}</div>`:'<div class="v90-empty">Nothing to count yet. Start a lesson or try one problem. Your work will show up here.</div>'}<p class="v90-muted">Older course versions use different scoring rules. This overview does not add those scores into a misleading single “mastery” number. Progress is saved on this device; use the existing backup tool to transfer it.</p>`;

 try{
  const read=k=>{try{return JSON.parse(localStorage.getItem(k)||'{}')}catch{return {}}};
  const c=read('studytable_v91_chess'),s=read('studytable_v91_spanish'),p=read('studytable_v91_piano'),v=read('studytable_v92_verbs','studytable_v95_skills');
  const chess=Array.isArray(c.clean)?c.clean.length:0,cards=Object.keys(s.cards||{}).length,verbs=Object.keys(v.items||{}).length,rounds=Array.isArray(p.rounds)?p.rounds.length:0;
  if(chess+cards+verbs+rounds){const node=document.createElement('section');node.className='v90-metric';node.innerHTML='<h3>Daily practice studio</h3><p>Chess: '+chess+' puzzles solved without help · Spanish: '+cards+' cards reviewed · Verbs: '+verbs+' forms practiced · Piano: '+rounds+' rounds</p><p>These are different kinds of practice, not a combined score.</p>';
   const launch=document.createElement('button');launch.type='button';launch.textContent='Open practice';launch.onclick=()=>{closeDashboard();window.studyTablePractice?.('today')};node.appendChild(launch);root.appendChild(node)}
 }catch(e){console.warn('Practice overview unavailable',e)}
 root.querySelectorAll('[data-v90-sub]').forEach(b=>b.onclick=()=>{closeDashboard();subjectOpen(b.dataset.v90Sub)});
}
let backFocus=null;
function openDashboard(){const node=$('v90Panel');if(!node)return;backFocus=document.activeElement;dashboard();node.hidden=false;document.body.classList.add('v90-dialog-open');$('v90Close')?.focus()}
function closeDashboard(){const node=$('v90Panel');if(!node)return;node.hidden=true;document.body.classList.remove('v90-dialog-open');backFocus?.focus?.()}
function notice(message){let node=$('v90Notice');if(!node){node=document.createElement('p');node.id='v90Notice';node.setAttribute('role','status');node.className='v90-notice';document.body.append(node)}node.textContent=message;node.hidden=false}

function backup(){
 const records={};for(const k of BACKUP_KEYS){try{const raw=localStorage.getItem(k);if(raw!==null)records[k]=JSON.parse(raw)}catch{}}

 const msg=$('v90BackupStatus');if(msg)msg.textContent='Your progress file has been prepared.';
}
async function restore(file){

 try{const result=await window.studyTableV94SafeRestore.restore(file);

  dashboard();const saved=$('v90Saved');if(saved)saved.textContent=summarize();
 }catch(err){if(msg)msg.textContent='Could not restore: '+String(err.message||err)}
}

let tab='today';
const dstr=()=>{const d=new Date();return [d.getFullYear(),String(d.getMonth()+1).padStart(2,'0'),String(d.getDate()).padStart(2,'0')].join('-')};
function tdy(){let t=jp(rd(KT),null);if(!t||t.d!==dstr())t={d:dstr(),chess:0,cards:0,verbs:0,rounds:0,cardIds:[]};if(t.schema!==93){t={d:dstr(),schema:93,chess:0,cards:0,verbs:0,rounds:0,cardIds:[]}}return t}
function bump(f,uniqueId){const t=tdy();
  if(f==='cards'){
    if(uniqueId===undefined)return;
    if(!Array.isArray(t.cardIds))t.cardIds=[];
    if(t.cardIds.includes(String(uniqueId)))return;
    t.cardIds.push(String(uniqueId));t.cards=Math.min(8,t.cardIds.length);
  }else t[f]=Math.min(1,(t[f]||0)+1);
  wr(KT,JSON.stringify(t));
}

});

/* ---------- Today ---------- */
function today(){
  const t=tdy(),items=[
 ['chess','Solve one new chess puzzle without help',t.chess,1,'chess'],
 ['cards','Write 8 Spanish answers correctly on the first try',t.cards,8,'spanish'],
 ['verbs','Get at least 7/10 verb forms right without help',t.verbs||0,1,'verbs'],
 ['rounds','Get at least 6/10 notes right by listening',t.rounds,1,'piano']
 ];
  const done=items.filter(i=>i[2]>=i[3]).length;
  $('v91Body').innerHTML='<div class="v91-note"><b>Four small things to practice.</b> A check means you met the target, not that you mastered the subject. No streaks, no deadlines.</div>'
   +items.map(i=>'<div class="v91-row" style="justify-content:space-between;border:1px solid #d2dfd5;border-radius:10px;padding:10px 12px"><span>'+(i[2]>=i[3]?'✅':'⬜')+' '+i[1]+' <small>('+Math.min(i[2],i[3])+'/'+i[3]+')</small></span><button class="v91-b" data-go="'+i[4]+'" type="button">'+(i[2]>=i[3]?'More':'Go')+'</button></div>').join('')
   +'<div class="v91-fb">'+(done===4?'<span class="v91-ok">All four targets reached today.</span>':done+' of 4 targets reached. Practice that needed help still counts as practice.')+'</div>'
   +'<div class="v91-row"><button class="v91-b p" id="v91Cont" type="button">Continue my lesson</button><button class="v91-b" id="v93ReviewFromToday" type="button">What needs practice?</button></div>';
  document.querySelectorAll('#v91Body [data-go]').forEach(b=>b.onclick=()=>{tab=b.dataset.go;show()});
  $('v91Cont').onclick=()=>{dlg.close();const c=$('v90Continue');if(c)c.click()};$('v93ReviewFromToday').onclick=()=>{tab='review';show()};
}

/* ---------- Review: short, honest progress ---------- */
function review(){
 const c=jp(rd(KC),{}),s=jp(rd(KS),{}),v=jp(rd(KV),{}),p=jp(rd(KP),{});
 const clean=Array.isArray(c.clean)?c.clean.length:0;
 const practiced=Array.isArray(c.solved)?c.solved.length:0;
 const cards=Object.values(s.cards||{}),typed=cards.filter(x=>x.typedClean>0).length,need=cards.filter(x=>x.lastMissed).length;
 const forms=Object.values(v.items||{}),strong=forms.filter(x=>x.clean>0).length,weak=forms.filter(x=>(x.misses||0)>0).length;
 const recent=(Array.isArray(p.history)?p.history:[]).filter(x=>x.mode==='listen');
 const last=recent.at(-1),listening=recent.length;
 $('v91Body').innerHTML='<div class="v91-card" style="text-align:left"><div class="big">What to practice next</div><p class="v91-note">A useful record of actual attempts. Different subjects are not combined into a made-up overall score.</p></div>'+
 '<div class="v93-review-grid">'+
 '<div class="v93-review-item"><strong>Chess</strong><p>'+clean+' of 20 puzzles solved without help; '+Math.max(0,practiced-clean)+' solved with help or on replay.</p><button class="v91-b" data-go="chess">Practice an unsolved position</button></div>'+
 '<div class="v93-review-item"><strong>Spanish cards</strong><p>'+typed+' cards written correctly on the first try; '+need+' cards missed at least once.</p><button class="v91-b" data-go="spanish">Write Spanish answers</button></div>'+
 '<div class="v93-review-item"><strong>Verb forms</strong><p>'+strong+' of 80 forms answered cleanly at least once; '+weak+' forms missed or assisted.</p><button class="v91-b" data-go="verbs">Review verb forms</button></div>'+
 '<div class="v93-review-item"><strong>Piano listening</strong><p>'+listening+' rounds recorded.'+(last?' Last score: '+last.correct+'/10.':' Try listening to ten notes.')+'</p><button class="v91-b" data-go="piano">Practice listening</button></div></div>'+
 '<p class="v91-note">A correct answer once is a start, not permanent mastery. An answer seen with a hint or a model is not an independent success.</p>';
 $('v91Body').querySelectorAll('[data-go]').forEach(b=>b.onclick=()=>{tab=b.dataset.go;if(tab==='spanish'){rev=true;flipped=false}if(tab==='piano')reset(false,'listen');show()});
}

/* ---------- Chess: find the checkmate ---------- */
const GL={K:'♔',Q:'♕',R:'♖',B:'♗',N:'♘',P:'♙',k:'♚',q:'♛',r:'♜',b:'♝',n:'♞',p:'♟'};
let cs=jp(rd(KC),{solved:[]});if(!Array.isArray(cs.solved))cs.solved=[];if(!Array.isArray(cs.clean))cs.clean=[];if(!cs.helpDays||typeof cs.helpDays!=='object')cs.helpDays={};
let pi=-1,sel=null,hintSq=null,fb='',bd=null,bdI=-1,stage=0,curM=[],cleanAttempt=true;
const sqName=(f,r)=>'abcdefgh'[f]+(r+1);
function parse(fen){const[p,t]=fen.split(' ');const b={};p.split('/').forEach((row,r)=>{let f=0;for(const ch of row){if(/\d/.test(ch))f+=+ch;else{b[sqName(f,7-r)]=ch;f++}}});return{b,t}}
function applyMv(m){const pc=bd[m.slice(0,2)];delete bd[m.slice(0,2)];bd[m.slice(2,4)]=pc}
function mateList(p){return p.kind==='m2'?(stage===0?Object.keys(p.keys):curM):p.mates}
function chess(){
  if(pi<0){pi=PUZ.findIndex(p=>!cs.solved.includes(p.id));if(pi<0)pi=0}
  const p=PUZ[pi],{b:b0,t}=parse(p.fen);
  if(bdI!==pi){bd=b0;bdI=pi;stage=0;curM=[];cleanAttempt=cs.helpDays[p.id]!==dstr()}
  const b=bd,flip=t==='b',white=t==='w',m2=p.kind==='m2';
  const files=flip?[7,6,5,4,3,2,1,0]:[0,1,2,3,4,5,6,7],ranks=flip?[0,1,2,3,4,5,6,7]:[7,6,5,4,3,2,1,0];
  let sq='';
  for(const r of ranks)for(const f of files){const n=sqName(f,r),pc=b[n]||'',light=(f+r)%2===1;
    sq+='<button class="v91-sq '+(light?'l':'d')+(sel===n?' sel':'')+(hintSq===n?' hint':'')+'" data-sq="'+n+'" aria-label="'+n+(pc?' '+pc:' empty')+'" type="button">'+(pc?GL[pc]:'')+'</button>'}
  $('v91Body').innerHTML='<div class="v91-note"><b>'+(stage===2?'Checkmate — puzzle complete.':m2?(stage===0?'Mate in two: find the first move.':'Now deliver checkmate.'):'Find the checkmate in one move.')+'</b> '+(white?'White':'Black')+' to move. Tap a piece, then the square to move it to.</div><div><b>'+esc(p.name)+'</b> · puzzle '+(pi+1)+' of '+PUZ.length+' · solved '+cs.solved.length+' · without help '+cs.clean.length+'</div><div class="v91-board" role="group" aria-label="Chess board">'+sq+'</div><div class="v91-fb" id="v91Fb" aria-live="polite">'+fb+'</div><div class="v91-row"><button class="v91-b" id="v91Hint" type="button">Hint</button><button class="v91-b" id="v91Show" type="button">Show answer</button><button class="v91-b" id="v91Reset" type="button">Restart</button><button class="v91-b p" id="v91Next" type="button">Next puzzle</button></div>';
  $('v91Body').querySelector('.v91-board').addEventListener('keydown',e=>{
    if(!['ArrowUp','ArrowDown','ArrowLeft','ArrowRight'].includes(e.key))return;
    e.preventDefault();const buttons=[...$('v91Body').querySelectorAll('[data-sq]')];
    const at=buttons.indexOf(document.activeElement);if(at<0)return;
    const r=Math.floor(at/8),c=at%8;const nextRow=Math.min(7,Math.max(0,r+(e.key==='ArrowDown'?1:e.key==='ArrowUp'?-1:0)));
    const nextCol=Math.min(7,Math.max(0,c+(e.key==='ArrowRight'?1:e.key==='ArrowLeft'?-1:0)));
    buttons[at].tabIndex=-1;buttons[nextRow*8+nextCol].tabIndex=0;buttons[nextRow*8+nextCol].focus();
  });
  document.querySelectorAll('#v91Body [data-sq]').forEach(e=>e.onclick=()=>{
    if(stage===2)return;const n=e.dataset.sq,pc=b[n];
    if(!sel){if(pc&&((pc===pc.toUpperCase())===white)){sel=n;hintSq=null;fb='';chess()}return}
    if(n===sel){sel=null;chess();return}
    if(pc&&((pc===pc.toUpperCase())===white)){sel=n;chess();return}
    const mv=sel+n;sel=null;hintSq=null;
    if(m2&&stage===0){
      const branches=DEFENSES[p.id]?.[mv];
      if(branches?.length){const defense=branches[Math.floor(Math.random()*branches.length)];applyMv(mv);applyMv(defense.reply);stage=1;curM=defense.mates;fb='<span class="v91-ok">That forces mate. Black chose '+defense.reply.slice(0,2)+' → '+defense.reply.slice(2)+'. Find your finishing move.</span>'}
      else {cleanAttempt=false;fb='<span class="v91-bad">That move does not force mate. Check the squares Black can still escape to.</span>'}
    }else if(mateList(p).includes(mv)){
      applyMv(mv);stage=2;const clean=cleanAttempt&&cs.helpDays[p.id]!==dstr();fb='<span class="v91-ok">Checkmate. '+esc(p.hint)+'</span> '+(clean?'<span class="v91-ok">Solved without help.</span>':'<span class="v91-note">Good practice. This was a guided attempt.</span>');
      if(!cs.solved.includes(p.id))cs.solved.push(p.id);if(clean&&!cs.clean.includes(p.id)){cs.clean.push(p.id);bump('chess')}wr(KC,JSON.stringify(cs))
    }else {cleanAttempt=false;fb='<span class="v91-bad">That isn’t checkmate. Look for a check where the king has no safe squares.</span>'}
    chess()});
  $('v91Hint').onclick=()=>{if(stage===2)return;cleanAttempt=false;cs.helpDays[p.id]=dstr();wr(KC,JSON.stringify(cs));hintSq=mateList(p)[0].slice(0,2);fb='Hint: start with the marked piece.';sel=null;chess()};
  $('v91Show').onclick=()=>{if(stage===2)return;cleanAttempt=false;cs.helpDays[p.id]=dstr();wr(KC,JSON.stringify(cs));const m=mateList(p)[0];fb='Answer: <b>'+m.slice(0,2)+' → '+m.slice(2)+'</b>. '+esc(p.hint)+' This attempt won’t count as unaided.';hintSq=m.slice(0,2);sel=null;chess()};
  $('v91Reset').onclick=()=>{if(!cleanAttempt)cs.helpDays[p.id]=dstr();bdI=-1;sel=null;hintSq=null;fb='';chess()};
  $('v91Next').onclick=()=>{const N=PUZ.length;let nx=-1;for(let k=1;k<=N;k++){const j=(pi+k)%N;if(!cs.solved.includes(PUZ[j].id)){nx=j;break}}pi=nx<0?(pi+1)%N:nx;bdI=-1;sel=null;hintSq=null;fb='';chess()};
}

/* ---------- Spanish: spaced cards (Leitner) ---------- */
let ss=jp(rd(KS),{cards:{}});if(!ss.cards||typeof ss.cards!=='object')ss.cards={};
const DAYS=[0,1,3,7,16],DAY=864e5;
let q=[],qi=0,flipped=false,rev=true,cardWrong=false,cardRevealed=false,cardMessage='';
const todayS=dstr;
function buildQ(){const now=Date.now();const allowed=Math.max(0,16-(ss.nd&&ss.nd.d===todayS()?ss.nd.n:0));const due=[],fresh=[];CARDS.forEach((c,i)=>{const s=ss.cards[i];if(!s)fresh.push(i);else if(s.d<=now)due.push(i)});due.sort((a,b)=>ss.cards[a].d-ss.cards[b].d);q=due.slice(0,12).concat(fresh.slice(0,Math.min(allowed,Math.max(8,16-due.length))));qi=0;flipped=false;cardWrong=false;cardRevealed=false;cardMessage=''}
function spanish(){
  if(!q.length||qi>=q.length)buildQ();
  const known=Object.values(ss.cards).filter(s=>s.b>=3).length;
  if(!q.length){$('v91Body').innerHTML='<div class="v91-card"><div class="big">All caught up 🎉</div><div class="ex">Nothing is due. Come back tomorrow, and the cards you found hard will return first.</div></div><p class="v91-note">'+known+' of '+CARDS.length+' cards reviewed several times.</p>';return}
  const i=q[qi],c=CARDS[i],front=rev?c[1]:c[0],back=rev?c[0]:c[1];
  $('v91Body').innerHTML='<div class="v91-note">Card '+(qi+1)+' of '+q.length+' · '+known+'/'+CARDS.length+' cards reviewed several times. '+(rev?'Write the Spanish, including accents.':'Say the English meaning before flipping; this mode is self-checked.')+'</div>'+
   '<div class="v91-card" id="v91C"><div class="big">'+esc(front)+'</div>'+(flipped?'<div class="big" style="margin-top:12px;color:#245e49">'+esc(back)+'</div><div class="ex">'+esc(c[2])+'</div>':'')+'</div>'+
   (rev&&!flipped?'<form id="v93CardForm" class="v92-verb-form"><label for="v93CardAnswer">Type the Spanish answer</label><div class="v91-row"><input id="v93CardAnswer" type="text" lang="es" autocapitalize="off" autocomplete="off" spellcheck="false" aria-describedby="v93CardFeedback" placeholder="Write it in Spanish"><button class="v91-b p" type="submit">Check</button></div></form>':'')+
   '<div id="v93CardFeedback" class="v91-note" role="status" aria-live="polite">'+cardMessage+'</div>'+
   '<div class="v91-row">'+(flipped?'<button class="v91-b" id="v91Again" type="button">Needs practice</button><button class="v91-b p" id="v91Good" type="button">I knew it (self-check)</button>':'<button class="v91-b" id="v91Flip" type="button">Show answer</button>')+
   '<button class="v91-b" id="v91Dir" type="button">'+(rev?'Switch to spoken self-review':'Switch to typed Spanish')+'</button>'+(window.speechSynthesis?'<button class="v91-b" id="v91Say" type="button" aria-label="Hear the Spanish">Hear Spanish</button>':'')+'</div>';
  const grade=(ok,verified=false)=>{
    if(!ss.cards[i]){ss.nd=(ss.nd&&ss.nd.d===todayS())?ss.nd:{d:todayS(),n:0};ss.nd.n++}
    const s=ss.cards[i]||{b:0,d:0};const remembered=verified||(!rev&&ok);s.b=remembered?Math.min(4,s.b+1):0;s.d=Date.now()+(remembered?DAYS[s.b]*DAY:15*60*1000);
    if(verified){s.typedClean=(s.typedClean||0)+1;s.lastTypedClean=dstr()}
    if(!ok||cardWrong)s.lastMissed=dstr();ss.cards[i]=s;wr(KS,JSON.stringify(ss));
    if(verified&&ok)bump('cards',i);
    qi++;flipped=false;cardWrong=false;cardRevealed=false;cardMessage='';if(!ok)q.push(i);spanish();
  };
  if(flipped){$('v91Again').onclick=()=>grade(false);$('v91Good').onclick=()=>grade(true,false)}
  else{
    $('v91Flip').onclick=()=>{cardRevealed=true;flipped=true;cardMessage='This was a review, not a checked recall.';spanish()};
    const form=$('v93CardForm');if(form){form.onsubmit=e=>{
      e.preventDefault();const raw=$('v93CardAnswer').value;
      const clean=s=>String(s).normalize('NFC').toLocaleLowerCase('es').replace(/[¿?¡!.,;…]/g,'').trim().replace(/\s+/g,' ');
      const answers=c[0].split(/\s+\/\s+/).map(clean);
      if(answers.includes(clean(raw))){const verified=!cardWrong&&!cardRevealed;grade(true,verified)}
      else{cardWrong=true;cardMessage='<span class="v91-bad">Not quite. Check the word and any written accent, or show the example.</span>';const note=$('v93CardFeedback');if(note)note.innerHTML=cardMessage}
    }}
  }
  $('v91Dir').onclick=()=>{rev=!rev;flipped=false;cardWrong=false;cardRevealed=false;cardMessage='';spanish()};
  const say=$('v91Say');if(say)say.onclick=()=>{try{const u=new SpeechSynthesisUtterance(c[0]);u.lang='es-ES';speechSynthesis.cancel();speechSynthesis.speak(u)}catch{}};

}

/* ---------- Piano: note reading ---------- */
let ps=jp(rd(KP),{rounds:[]});if(!Array.isArray(ps.rounds))ps.rounds=[];
const NAMES=['C','C♯','D','D♯','E','F','F♯','G','G♯','A','A♯','B'];
const WH=[0,2,4,5,7,9,11,12],BL={1:1,3:2,6:4,8:5,10:6};
let ac=null,pr={n:0,ok:0,target:null,sharps:false,lock:false,msg:'',done:false,mode:'read'};
function tone(semi){try{ac=ac||new(window.AudioContext||window.webkitAudioContext)();const o=ac.createOscillator(),g=ac.createGain();o.type='triangle';o.frequency.value=261.63*Math.pow(2,semi/12);g.gain.setValueAtTime(.001,ac.currentTime);g.gain.exponentialRampToValueAtTime(.35,ac.currentTime+.02);g.gain.exponentialRampToValueAtTime(.001,ac.currentTime+.9);o.connect(g).connect(ac.destination);o.start();o.stop(ac.currentTime+1)}catch{}}
function pick(){const pool=pr.sharps?[...Array(13).keys()]:WH;let t;do{t=pool[Math.floor(Math.random()*pool.length)]}while(t===pr.target);pr.target=t}
const tname=s=>NAMES[s%12]+(s===12?' (high C)':'');
function reset(sharps,mode){pr.sharps=sharps;if(mode)pr.mode=mode;pr.n=0;pr.ok=0;pr.msg='';pr.done=false;pr.target=null;pick();if(pr.mode==='listen')setTimeout(()=>{if(tab==='piano'&&pr.mode==='listen')tone(pr.target)},120)}
function piano(){
  if(pr.target===null)pick();
  const best=ps.rounds.reduce((m,r)=>Math.max(m,Number(r)||0),0);
  let keys='';const W=100/8;
  WH.forEach((s,i)=>keys+='<button class="v91-w" data-s="'+s+'" style="left:'+i*W+'%;width:'+W+'%" aria-label="'+tname(s)+'" type="button"></button>');
  if(pr.sharps)Object.entries(BL).forEach(([s,pos])=>keys+='<button class="v91-k" data-s="'+s+'" style="left:'+(pos*W-W*0.30)+'%;width:'+W*0.6+'%" aria-label="'+NAMES[s]+'" type="button"></button>');
  $('v91Body').innerHTML='<div class="v91-note">Find the key, then hear it. Rounds of 10. '+(best?'Best: '+best+'/10.':'')+'</div><div class="v91-row"><button class="v91-b'+(pr.sharps?'':' p')+'" id="v91W" type="button">White keys</button><button class="v91-b'+(pr.sharps?' p':'')+'" id="v91S" type="button">With sharps</button><button class="v91-b'+(pr.mode==='read'?' p':'')+'" id="v91MR" type="button">Read</button><button class="v91-b'+(pr.mode==='listen'?' p':'')+'" id="v91ML" type="button">Listen</button></div><div class="v91-card" style="min-height:90px"><div class="big">'+(pr.done?'Round finished':(pr.mode==='listen'?'Which key did you hear?':'Find: '+esc(tname(pr.target))))+'</div>'+(pr.mode==='listen'&&!pr.done?'<div class="v91-row" style="justify-content:center"><button class="v91-b" id="v91Play" type="button">▶ Hear it again</button></div>':'')+'</div><div class="v91-keys" id="v91Keys">'+keys+'</div><div class="v91-fb" id="v91Fb" aria-live="polite">'+pr.msg+'</div><div class="v91-note">Question '+Math.min(pr.n+1,10)+' of 10 · correct so far: '+pr.ok+'</div>';
  $('v91W').onclick=()=>{reset(false);piano()};
  $('v91MR').onclick=()=>{reset(pr.sharps,'read');piano()};$('v91ML').onclick=()=>{reset(pr.sharps,'listen');piano()};
  const pl=$('v91Play');if(pl)pl.onclick=()=>tone(pr.target);
  $('v91S').onclick=()=>{reset(true);piano()};
  document.querySelectorAll('#v91Keys [data-s]').forEach(k=>k.onclick=()=>{
    if(pr.lock)return;const s=+k.dataset.s;tone(s);
    if(pr.done){reset(pr.sharps);piano();return}
    const t=pr.target,ok=s===t;
    k.classList.add(ok?'good':'bad');
    if(!ok){const g=document.querySelector('#v91Keys [data-s="'+t+'"]');if(g)g.classList.add('good')}
    pr.lock=true;
    setTimeout(()=>{pr.lock=false;pr.n++;if(ok)pr.ok++;
      if(pr.n>=10){ps.rounds.push(pr.ok);ps.rounds=ps.rounds.slice(-60);if(!Array.isArray(ps.history))ps.history=[];ps.history.push({day:dstr(),mode:pr.mode,correct:pr.ok,total:10});ps.history=ps.history.slice(-90);ps.lastMode=pr.mode;wr(KP,JSON.stringify(ps));pr.msg='<span class="v91-ok">Round done: '+pr.ok+'/10.</span> '+(pr.mode==='listen'&&pr.ok<6?'Try another listening round for today’s target.':'Tap any key to start another.');pr.done=true;if(pr.mode==='listen'&&pr.ok>=6)bump('rounds')}
      else{pr.msg=ok?'<span class="v91-ok">Yes — '+esc(tname(t))+'.</span>':'<span class="v91-bad">That was '+esc(tname(s))+'. The target was '+esc(tname(t))+' (shown in green).</span>';pick();if(pr.mode==='listen')setTimeout(()=>tone(pr.target),350)}
      piano()},ok?350:1200)});
}


/* ---------- Spanish: write the verb yourself ---------- */
// Five personal forms for each of 16 common verbs. Each form is authored and checked strictly.
const VP=['yo','tú','él / ella','nosotros','ellos / ellas'];
const VERB_DATA=[
 ['hablar','to speak',['hablo','hablas','habla','hablamos','hablan'],'Regular -ar: remove -ar; add -o, -as, -a, -amos, -an.'],
 ['comer','to eat',['como','comes','come','comemos','comen'],'Regular -er: -o, -es, -e, -emos, -en.'],
 ['vivir','to live',['vivo','vives','vive','vivimos','viven'],'Regular -ir: -o, -es, -e, -imos, -en.'],
 ['trabajar','to work',['trabajo','trabajas','trabaja','trabajamos','trabajan'],'Regular -ar: the same endings as hablar.'],
 ['aprender','to learn',['aprendo','aprendes','aprende','aprendemos','aprenden'],'Regular -er: the same endings as comer.'],
 ['escribir','to write',['escribo','escribes','escribe','escribimos','escriben'],'Regular -ir: the same endings as vivir.'],
 ['ser','to be (identity)',['soy','eres','es','somos','son'],'Ser is irregular; its forms must be learned individually. Use it for identity or a lasting characteristic.'],
 ['estar','to be (state/location)',['estoy','estás','está','estamos','están'],'Estar changes in the tú, él/ella and ellos/ellas forms: the stressed á takes an accent.'],
 ['ir','to go',['voy','vas','va','vamos','van'],'Ir is irregular: voy, vas, va, vamos, van.'],
 ['tener','to have',['tengo','tienes','tiene','tenemos','tienen'],'The yo form is tengo; the stem changes e → ie except in nosotros.'],
 ['hacer','to do/make',['hago','haces','hace','hacemos','hacen'],'The yo form is hago; the other common forms follow hacer’s stem.'],
 ['querer','to want',['quiero','quieres','quiere','queremos','quieren'],'The stem changes e → ie except in nosotros.'],
 ['poder','to be able to',['puedo','puedes','puede','podemos','pueden'],'The stem changes o → ue except in nosotros.'],
 ['decir','to say/tell',['digo','dices','dice','decimos','dicen'],'The yo form is digo; other common forms use dic-.'],
 ['venir','to come',['vengo','vienes','viene','venimos','vienen'],'The yo form is vengo; the stem changes e → ie except in nosotros.'],
 ['saber','to know (a fact)',['sé','sabes','sabe','sabemos','saben'],'The yo form is sé with an accent; se without an accent is a different word.']
];
const VF=VERB_DATA.flatMap((v,i)=>VP.map((pron,j)=>({id:i+'-'+j,verb:v[0],meaning:v[1],pron,answer:v[2][j],why:v[3]})));
let verbSave=jp(rd(KV),{items:{},rounds:[],last:[]});
if(!verbSave.items||typeof verbSave.items!=='object')verbSave.items={};
if(!Array.isArray(verbSave.rounds))verbSave.rounds=[];
let vq=[],vi=0,vtries=0,vaided=false,vfeedback='',vrevealed=false,vdone=false,vscore=0,vlastId='';
const plain=s=>String(s).normalize('NFD').replace(/[\u0300-\u036f]/g,'').toLowerCase().trim().replace(/\s+/g,' ');
const normalize=s=>String(s).trim().toLocaleLowerCase('es').replace(/\s+/g,' ');
function nextVerbRound(){
 const now=Date.now();const pool=VF.map(x=>{
   const t=verbSave.items[x.id]||{},due=!t.due||t.due<=now,miss=t.misses||0;
   const weight=(due?8:1)+Math.min(12,miss*3)+(t.clean?0:2)+Math.random()*4;
   return {x,weight}
 });
 // Due and missed forms return first. Never repeat the same form in a single round.
 pool.sort((a,b)=>b.weight-a.weight);vq=pool.slice(0,10).map(y=>y.x);
 vi=0;vtries=0;vaided=false;vfeedback='';vrevealed=false;vdone=false;vscore=0;
}
function saveVerbResult(x,clean){
 const item=verbSave.items[x.id]||{attempts:0,misses:0,clean:0};
 item.attempts++;if(!clean)item.misses++;
 if(clean)item.clean++;
 item.last=Date.now();item.due=Date.now()+(clean?864e5*3:864e5);
 verbSave.items[x.id]=item;
 wr(KV,JSON.stringify(verbSave));
}
function finishVerb(x,clean,revealed){
 if(vdone)return;
 vdone=true; if(clean)vscore++;
 saveVerbResult(x,clean);
 vfeedback='<span class="v91-ok">'+(clean?'Exactly right, without help.':revealed?'Here is the form to remember.':'That is the right form. Keep the accent and spelling in mind.')+'</span> <strong>'+esc(x.pron)+' '+esc(x.answer)+'</strong><div class="v92-verb-why">'+esc(x.why)+'</div>';
 verbs();
}
function finishRound(){
 const r={date:dstr(),clean:vscore,total:10};
 verbSave.rounds.push(r);verbSave.rounds=verbSave.rounds.slice(-80);
 wr(KV,JSON.stringify(verbSave));if(vscore>=7)bump('verbs');
 vi=10;verbs();
}
function checkVerb(){
 if(vdone||vi>=10)return;
 const x=vq[vi],value=$('v92VerbInput')?.value||'';
 if(!value.trim()){vfeedback='<span class="v91-bad">Type a form first. If you’re stuck, use a hint.</span>';verbs(value);return}
 vtries++;
 if(normalize(value)===normalize(x.answer)){
   finishVerb(x,!vaided&&vtries===1,false);return;
 }
 vaided=true;
 if(plain(value)===plain(x.answer)){
   vfeedback='<span class="v91-bad">The letters are right. Check the written accent.</span> '+esc(x.why);
 }else{
   vfeedback='<span class="v91-bad">Not yet. Look at the pronoun and the verb ending.</span> '+esc(x.why);
 }
 if(vtries>=2){vfeedback+=' <span class="v92-soft">You can try once more or reveal the answer.</span>'}
 verbs(value);
}
function verbs(input=''){
 if(!vq.length)nextVerbRound();
 if(vi>=10){
   const total=Object.keys(verbSave.items).length,clean=Object.values(verbSave.items).filter(x=>x.clean>0).length;
   $('v91Body').innerHTML='<div class="v91-card"><div class="big">Round complete</div><p>'+vscore+' of 10 correct without help. '+(vscore>=7?'Today’s verb target is met.':'That was useful practice. Aim for 7 clean answers in another round.')+' You have practiced '+total+' of 80 forms; '+clean+' solved independently at least once.</p></div><div class="v91-row"><button type="button" id="v92VerbAgain" class="v91-b p">Another round</button><button type="button" id="v92VerbHome" class="v91-b">Today</button></div>';
   $('v92VerbAgain').onclick=()=>{nextVerbRound();verbs()};$('v92VerbHome').onclick=()=>{tab='today';show()};return
 }
 const x=vq[vi];
 $('v91Body').innerHTML='<div class="v91-note">Present tense · '+(vi+1)+' of 10. Type the verb for the person shown. Say it aloud if you like.</div><div class="v91-card v92-verb-card"><div class="v92-verb-label">'+esc(x.pron)+'</div><div class="big">'+esc(x.verb)+'</div><div class="ex">'+esc(x.meaning)+'</div></div>'+(!vdone?'<form id="v92VerbForm" class="v92-verb-form"><label for="v92VerbInput">Write the present-tense form</label><div class="v91-row"><input id="v92VerbInput" type="text" lang="es" spellcheck="false" autocapitalize="off" autocomplete="off" inputmode="text" placeholder="Type your answer" aria-describedby="v92VerbFeedback"><button type="submit" class="v91-b p">Check</button></div></form>':'')+'<div class="v91-fb" role="status" aria-live="polite" id="v92VerbFeedback">'+vfeedback+'</div><div class="v91-row">'+(vdone?'<button type="button" id="v92VerbNext" class="v91-b p">'+(vi===9?'Finish round':'Next form')+'</button>':'<button type="button" class="v91-b" id="v92VerbHint">Hint</button><button type="button" class="v91-b" id="v92VerbShow">Show answer</button>')+'</div>';
 if(vdone){$('v92VerbNext').onclick=()=>{if(vi===9)finishRound();else{vi++;vfeedback='';vdone=false;vaided=false;vtries=0;verbs()}}}
 else{
  const field=$('v92VerbInput');field.value=input;field.focus();
  $('v92VerbForm').onsubmit=e=>{e.preventDefault();checkVerb()};
  $('v92VerbHint').onclick=()=>{vaided=true;vfeedback='Think about the '+esc(x.pron)+' form. '+esc(x.why);verbs(field.value)};
  $('v92VerbShow').onclick=()=>{vaided=true;vrevealed=true;finishVerb(x,false,true)};
 }
}

window.studyTableV92={open,VERB_COUNT:VF.length,CHESS_COUNT:PUZ.length,defenses:DEFENSES,localDay:dstr};window.studyTableV93={open,review,localDay:dstr,cardCount:CARDS.length};
})();

<script id="v94-safe-restore">
/* Study Table V94 — conservative and validated progress merge.
   Both old backup entry points use this one import routine. */
(() => {'use strict';
const KNOWN = new Set([
 'studytable_v35_progress','studytable_v54_teachback','studytable_v51_notes','studytable_v57_practice',
 'studytable_v62_discoveries','studytable_v63_loops','studytable_v64_think_aloud','studytable_v65_sessions',
 'studytable_v65_verbs','studytable_v66_conversations','studytable_v67_piano','studytable_v70_lab',
 'studytable_v72_chess','studytable_v73_coach','studytable_v74_diagnostics','studytable_v76_coach',
 'studytable_v77_compass','studytable_v78_coach','studytable_v81_written','studytable_v83_cases',
 'studytable_v84_case_notebook','studytable_v85_case_review','studytable_v86_practice','studytable_v89_guided',
 'studytable_v90_preferences','studytable_v56_review','studytable_v91_today','studytable_v91_chess',
 'studytable_v91_spanish','studytable_v91_piano','studytable_v92_verbs','studytable_v95_skills',
 'study-table-v19:v27:curriculum','study-table-v19:v23:lessons','studytable_v97_teacher','studytable:v100:recall','studytable_v98_recall','studytable:v102:mastery','studytable_v102_learning'
]);
const BAD = new Set(['__proto__','constructor','prototype']);
const object=x=>x!==null&&typeof x==='object'&&!Array.isArray(x);
const plain=x=>object(x)&&(Object.getPrototypeOf(x)===Object.prototype||Object.getPrototypeOf(x)===null);
let lastSnapshot=null;
function validate(value,depth=0,stats={nodes:0}) {
 if(++stats.nodes>60000||depth>14)throw Error('The backup contains too much nested data.');
 if(typeof value==='string'){
  if(value.length>50000||/<\/?[a-z][^>]*>/i.test(value)||/\b(?:javascript|data):\s*(?:text\/html|[^\s]+)/i.test(value))throw Error('The backup contains unsafe text.');
  return;
 }
 if(typeof value==='number'&&!Number.isFinite(value))throw Error('The backup contains an invalid number.');
 if(value===null||typeof value==='number'||typeof value==='boolean')return;
 if(Array.isArray(value)){
  if(value.length>5000)throw Error('The backup contains an oversized list.');
  for(const x of value)validate(x,depth+1,stats);return;
 }
 if(!plain(value))throw Error('The backup contains an invalid data type.');
 const keys=Object.keys(value);
 if(keys.length>5000)throw Error('The backup contains too many records.');
 for(const k of keys){if(BAD.has(k)||k.length>200||/<|>/.test(k))throw Error('The backup contains an unsafe record name.');validate(value[k],depth+1,stats)}
}
function schema(key,v){
 if(!plain(v))throw Error('Progress section '+key+' is not an object.');
 if(key==='studytable:v102:mastery'&&v.progress!==undefined&&!plain(v.progress))throw Error('Invalid skill challenge progress.');
 if(key==='studytable:v100:recall'&&v.cards!==undefined&&!plain(v.cards))throw Error('Invalid recall progress.');
 if(key==='studytable_v91_chess'){
  if(v.solved!==undefined&&(!Array.isArray(v.solved)||v.solved.some(x=>typeof x!=='string'||x.length>50)))throw Error('Invalid chess puzzle list.');
  if(v.clean!==undefined&&(!Array.isArray(v.clean)||v.clean.some(x=>typeof x!=='string'||x.length>50)))throw Error('Invalid chess clean list.');
  if(v.helpDays!==undefined&&!plain(v.helpDays))throw Error('Invalid chess help history.');
 }
 if(key==='studytable_v91_spanish'&&v.cards!==undefined&&!plain(v.cards))throw Error('Invalid Spanish card history.');
 if(key==='studytable_v92_verbs'&&v.items!==undefined&&!plain(v.items))throw Error('Invalid verb history.');
 if(key==='studytable_v95_skills'&&(v.events!==undefined&&!Array.isArray(v.events)||v.observations!==undefined&&!plain(v.observations)))throw Error('Invalid shared skill history.');
 if(key==='studytable_v91_piano'&&v.history!==undefined&&!Array.isArray(v.history))throw Error('Invalid piano history.');
 if(key==='studytable_v91_today'&&v.d!==undefined&&!/^\d{4}-\d\d-\d\d$/.test(v.d))throw Error('Invalid daily date.');
}
const canon=x=>JSON.stringify(x);
function merge(local,incoming,depth=0){
 if(depth>14)return local;
 if(plain(local)&&plain(incoming)){
  const result=Object.assign(Object.create(null),local);
  for(const [key,value] of Object.entries(incoming)){
   if(BAD.has(key))throw Error('Unsafe key.');
   result[key]=Object.hasOwn(local,key)?merge(local[key],value,depth+1):value;
  }
  return result;
 }
 if(Array.isArray(local)&&Array.isArray(incoming)){
  // Preserve all existing attempts. Add genuinely new imported records, never replace history.
  const result=local.slice(),seen=new Set(result.map(canon));
  for(const x of incoming){const mark=canon(x);if(!seen.has(mark)){result.push(x);seen.add(mark)}}
  return result;
 }
 // Conflicting scalar values cannot be safely reconciled by a generic importer.
 // Prefer the learner's current value; still import missing fields/records above.
 return local;
}
const clone=x=>JSON.parse(JSON.stringify(x));
async function restore(file){
 if(!file)throw Error('Choose a progress file.');
 if(file.size>5000000)throw Error('Progress backup exceeds 5 MB.');
 const payload=JSON.parse(await file.text());
 if(!plain(payload)||payload.format!=='studytable-progress'||![56,57].includes(payload.version)||!plain(payload.records))throw Error('Not a supported Study Table progress backup.');
 const keys=Object.keys(payload.records);
 if(!keys.length)throw Error('This progress file contains no records.');
 if(keys.some(k=>!KNOWN.has(k)))throw Error('The backup includes unknown progress sections. Nothing was changed.');
 const updates=[],before={};
 for(const key of keys){
  const imported=payload.records[key];validate(imported);schema(key,imported);
  const raw=localStorage.getItem(key);before[key]=raw;
  let output=imported;
  if(raw!==null){const local=JSON.parse(raw);validate(local);schema(key,local);output=merge(local,imported)}
  validate(output);schema(key,output);
  const json=JSON.stringify(output);
  if(json.length>1500000)throw Error('Merged '+key+' exceeds the safe size limit.');
  updates.push([key,json]);
 }
 // Preserve the previous values in the current browser session and allow one-click Undo.
 // Do not touch any storage key until every incoming section is validated.
 const snapshot=clone(before);
 try{sessionStorage.setItem('studytable_v94_before_restore',JSON.stringify(snapshot))}catch{}
 lastSnapshot=snapshot;
 try{for(const [key,value] of updates)localStorage.setItem(key,value)}
 catch(err){for(const [key,old] of updates.map(([key])=>[key,before[key]])){try{if(old===null)localStorage.removeItem(key);else localStorage.setItem(key,old)}catch{}}throw Error('Storage is full or unavailable; restore was rolled back. '+String(err?.message||''))}
 return {count:updates.length,message:`Merged ${updates.length} progress sections without replacing your existing work. Reload the page to refresh open exercises.`};
}
function undo(){
 let s=lastSnapshot;
 if(!s){try{s=JSON.parse(sessionStorage.getItem('studytable_v94_before_restore')||'null')}catch{}}
 if(!s||!plain(s))throw Error('No restore snapshot is available in this browser session.');
 for(const key of Object.keys(s))if(!KNOWN.has(key))throw Error('Invalid undo snapshot.');
 for(const [key,val] of Object.entries(s)){if(val===null)localStorage.removeItem(key);else localStorage.setItem(key,val)}
 lastSnapshot=null;try{sessionStorage.removeItem('studytable_v94_before_restore')}catch{}
 return 'The previous progress values have been restored. Reload the page to refresh open exercises.';
}
window.studyTableV94SafeRestore={restore,undo,known:[...KNOWN],validate};
})();

</script>

<script id="v95_skill_atlas">
/* Study Table V95 — one normalized, conservative view of skill evidence.
   Historical stores remain untouched for backward compatibility. New labs write here directly.
   Legacy practice is projected by stable source IDs, not added repeatedly on refresh. */
(()=>{'use strict';
const KEY='studytable_v95_skills',VERSION=1;
const SUBJECTS=['spanish','piano','chess','calculus','physics','medicine','history','economics','life'];
const LABEL={spanish:'Spanish',piano:'Piano',chess:'Chess',calculus:'Calculus',physics:'Physics',medicine:'Medicine',history:'History',economics:'Economics',life:'Life'};
const KNOW={
 'calculus.first-steps':'Calculus foundations','physics.first-steps':'Physics foundations','spanish.first-steps':'Practical Spanish','piano.first-steps':'Reading and playing notes','chess.first-steps':'Chess reasoning','medicine.first-steps':'Medical reasoning','history.first-steps':'Historical sources','economics.first-steps':'Economic choices','life.first-steps':'Everyday decisions',
 'calculus.slope':'Slope and rate of change','calculus.velocity':'Instantaneous velocity','calculus.area':'Area and accumulation','calculus.chain':'The chain rule','calculus.limit':'Limits and continuity','calculus.optimize':'Optimization','calculus.exponential':'Exponential growth','calculus.quadratic':'Quadratics',
 'physics.cart':'Forces and acceleration','physics.drop':'Free fall','physics.kettle':'Energy and power','physics.spring':'Springs and Hooke’s law','physics.circuit':'Electric circuits','physics.water':'Pressure in fluids','physics.collision':'Collisions and momentum','physics.heat':'Thermal energy',
 'calculus.derivative-lab':'Derivatives in motion','calculus.integral-lab':'Integration and distance','calculus.chain-lab':'How the chain rule works','calculus.garden-lab':'Optimizing an area','calculus.slope-lab':'Slope as rise over run',
 'physics.force-lab':'Net force and acceleration','physics.energy-lab':'Energy conservation','physics.circuit-lab':'Current, voltage and power','physics.fall-lab':'Speed and distance when falling','physics.momentum-lab':'Momentum in a collision',
 'calculus.earlier-course':'Full calculus continuation lessons','physics.earlier-course':'Full physics continuation lessons','calculus.earlier-foundations':'Earlier calculus foundations','physics.earlier-foundations':'Earlier physics foundations','calculus.earlier-experiments':'Graph-based derivative lab','physics.earlier-experiments':'Graph-based Newton lab',
 'chess.mate-one':'Mate in one','chess.mate-two':'Mate in two','spanish.cards':'Vocabulary recall','spanish.verbs':'Present-tense conjugation','spanish.past':'Past tenses','piano.ear':'Note listening','piano.melody':'Playing melodies','medicine.cases':'Medical case reasoning','history.cases':'Historical source reasoning'
};
const safe=(key)=>{try{const v=JSON.parse(localStorage.getItem(key)||'{}');return v&&typeof v==='object'&&!Array.isArray(v)?v:{}}catch{return {}}};
const isObj=v=>v!==null&&typeof v==='object'&&!Array.isArray(v);
const esc=s=>String(s??'').replace(/[&<>"']/g,c=>({'&':'&amp;','<':'&lt;','>':'&gt;','"':'&quot;',"'":'&#39;'}[c]));
function fresh(){return {version:VERSION,created:Date.now(),observations:{},events:[]}}
function read(){const x=safe(KEY);if(x.version!==VERSION||!isObj(x.observations)||!Array.isArray(x.events))return fresh();return x}
function persist(x){try{localStorage.setItem(KEY,JSON.stringify(x));return true}catch{return false}}
function observe(out,id,subject,skill,values){if(!SUBJECTS.includes(subject)||!id||!isObj(values))return;out[id]={subject,skill,label:KNOW[subject+'.'+skill]||skill,source:values.source||'Previous practice',attempts:Math.min(10000,Math.max(0,+values.attempts||0)),clean:Math.min(10000,Math.max(0,+values.clean||0)),helped:Math.min(10000,Math.max(0,+values.helped||0)),misses:Math.min(10000,Math.max(0,+values.misses||0)),last:+values.last||0,due:+values.due||0,exposure:!!values.exposure};}
function importLegacy(){const obs={};
 const guided=safe('studytable_v89_guided');for(const [key,val] of Object.entries(guided)){const [subject,skill]=key.split('/');if(!['calculus','physics','chess'].includes(subject)||!isObj(val))continue;observe(obs,'v89:'+key,subject,skill,{source:'Guided problems',attempts:val.attempts,clean:val.clean,helped:val.hints,misses:val.misses,last:val.lastCompleted,due:val.nextReview});}
 const verbs=safe('studytable_v92_verbs');for(const [id,v] of Object.entries(verbs.items||{})){if(!isObj(v))continue;observe(obs,'verb:'+id,'spanish','verbs',{source:'Verb drill',attempts:v.attempts,clean:v.clean,misses:v.misses,last:v.last,due:v.due});}
 const spanish=safe('studytable_v91_spanish');for(const [id,v] of Object.entries(spanish.cards||{})){if(!isObj(v))continue;observe(obs,'card:'+id,'spanish','cards',{source:'Vocabulary cards',attempts:Math.max(+v.typedClean||0,+v.b||0,1),clean:v.typedClean||0,misses:v.lastMissed?1:0,last:v.lastTypedClean?Date.parse(v.lastTypedClean)||0:0,due:v.d||0});}
 const chess=safe('studytable_v91_chess');for(const id of new Set([...(Array.isArray(chess.solved)?chess.solved:[]),...(Array.isArray(chess.clean)?chess.clean:[])])){if(typeof id!=='string'||id.length>50)continue;observe(obs,'chess:'+id,'chess',id.startsWith('t')?'mate-two':'mate-one',{source:'Chess puzzles',attempts:1,clean:Array.isArray(chess.clean)&&chess.clean.includes(id)?1:0});}
 const piano=safe('studytable_v91_piano');for(const [idx,v] of (Array.isArray(piano.rounds)?piano.rounds:[]).entries()){if(!isObj(v))continue;observe(obs,'piano-round:'+idx,'piano','ear',{source:'Piano listening',attempts:Math.min(10,+v.n||10),clean:v.mode==='listen'?Math.min(10,+v.ok||0):0,last:Date.parse(v.date||'')||0});}
 const studio=safe('studytable_v86_practice');for(const [category,items] of Object.entries(studio)){if(!isObj(items))continue;const subject=/^(spanish|past)$/.test(category)?'spanish':/^(melodies|ear|ears|piano)$/.test(category)?'piano':null;if(!subject)continue;for(const [id,v] of Object.entries(items)){if(!isObj(v))continue;const skill=subject==='spanish'?(category==='past'?'past':'verbs'):(category==='melodies'?'melody':'ear');observe(obs,'v86:'+category+':'+id,subject,skill,{source:'Spanish and piano studio',attempts:v.attempts||v.attempted||0,clean:(v.bestClean||v.clean===true||v.independent===true)?1:0,last:v.last||0});}}
 const cases=safe('studytable_v83_cases');for(const [id,v] of Object.entries(cases.records||{})){if(!isObj(v))continue;const subject=id.startsWith('hist-')?'history':id.startsWith('med-')?'medicine':null;if(!subject)continue;observe(obs,'case:'+id,subject,'cases',{source:'Case room',attempts:v.runs,clean:v.clean?1:0,last:v.last||0,due:v.due||0});}
 const earlier=safe('study-table-v19:v27:curriculum');
 for(const [key,v] of Object.entries(earlier)){
  const m=/^(calculus|physics):(\d+)$/.exec(key);if(!m||!isObj(v))continue;
  observe(obs,'v27:'+key,m[1],'earlier-course',{source:'Original continuation lessons (V27)',attempts:v.attempts||1,clean:v.independent?1:0,helped:v.independent?0:1,last:v.when||0,due:v.reviewAt||0,exposure:!!v.explored&&!v.independent});
 }
 const firsts=safe('study-table-v19:v23:lessons');
 for(const [key,v] of Object.entries(firsts)){
  const m=/^(calculus|physics):(\d+)$/.exec(key);if(!m||!isObj(v))continue;
  observe(obs,'v23:'+key,m[1],'earlier-foundations',{source:'Original guided foundations (V23)',attempts:0,clean:0,exposure:true,last:v.at||0,due:v.reviewAt||0});
 }
 const earlierLab=safe('studytable_v70_lab');
 for(const sub of ['calculus','physics']){
  const v=earlierLab[sub];if(!isObj(v))continue;
  observe(obs,'v71:'+sub,sub,'earlier-experiments',{source:'Original V71 graph lab',attempts:v.attempts||0,clean:v.independent||0,helped:Math.max(0,(v.correct||0)-(v.independent||0)),last:0});
 }
 const seen=safe('studytable_v35_progress');for(const [id,v] of Object.entries(seen)){if(!isObj(v)||!v.seen)continue;const subject=id.split(':')[0].toLowerCase();if(!SUBJECTS.includes(subject))continue;observe(obs,'lesson:'+id,subject,'lessons',{source:'Lessons visited',attempts:0,clean:0,last:+v.last||0,exposure:true});}
 return obs;
}
function refresh(){const store=read();store.observations=importLegacy();store.updated=Date.now();persist(store);return store}
function record(payload){const subject=String(payload?.subject||''),skill=String(payload?.skill||'');if(!SUBJECTS.includes(subject)||!/^[-\w]{1,60}$/.test(skill))return false;
 const item=String(payload.item||'').slice(0,80);if(!item)return false;
 const status=['independent','assisted','missed','revealed','practiced'].includes(payload.status)?payload.status:'practiced';
 const s=read(),last=s.events.filter(e=>e.item===item).at(-1);
 const ev={item,subject,skill,status,at:Date.now(),repeat:!!last};s.events.push(ev);s.events=s.events.slice(-1500);persist(s);return true;
}
function aggregate(){const s=refresh(),by={};for(const subj of SUBJECTS)by[subj]=[];
 const skills=new Map();const ensure=(subject,skill)=>{const id=subject+'.'+skill;if(!skills.has(id))skills.set(id,{id,subject,skill,label:KNOW[id]||skill,items:new Set(),independent:0,assisted:0,missed:0,exposed:0,last:0,due:0,sources:new Set()});return skills.get(id)};
 for(const [id,o] of Object.entries(s.observations)){const r=ensure(o.subject,o.skill);r.items.add(id);r.exposed+=o.exposure?1:0;r.independent+=o.clean;r.assisted+=Math.max(0,o.attempts-o.clean);r.missed+=o.misses;r.last=Math.max(r.last,o.last);if(o.due&&o.due<=Date.now()&&o.clean===0)r.due++;r.sources.add(o.source)}
 for(const e of s.events){if(!e||!SUBJECTS.includes(e.subject))continue;const r=ensure(e.subject,e.skill);r.items.add('new:'+e.item);if(e.status==='independent')r.independent++;else if(e.status==='missed')r.missed++;else r.assisted++;r.last=Math.max(r.last,e.at);r.sources.add('Interactive labs');}
 for(const r of skills.values()){r.status=r.due>0?'Ready to revisit':r.missed>0&&r.independent===0?'Needs another try':r.independent>0?'Clean answers recorded':r.assisted>0?'Practising with help':'Explored';r.sources=[...r.sources];r.items=r.items.size;by[r.subject].push(r)}
 return {by,skills:[...skills.values()],historical:Object.keys(s.observations).length,events:s.events.length,updated:s.updated};}
function choose(subject){const a=aggregate().by[subject]||[];const prioritized=a.slice().sort((x,y)=>(y.due>0)-(x.due>0)||(y.missed>0&&y.independent===0)-(x.missed>0&&x.independent===0)||x.independent-y.independent||x.items-y.items);return prioritized[0]||null}
let dialog=null,previousFocus=null,current='all';
function goto(subject){close();if(['calculus','physics'].includes(subject)&&window.studyTableV95Labs){window.studyTableV95Labs.open(subject);return}
 if(subject==='chess')window.studyTablePractice?.('chess');else if(subject==='spanish')window.studyTablePractice?.('verbs');else if(subject==='piano')window.studyTablePractice?.('piano');else if(typeof window.studyTableV90?.subjectOpen==='function')window.studyTableV90.subjectOpen(subject)}
function paint(){if(!dialog)return;const data=aggregate();const d=dialog.querySelector('#v95Content');const subjects=current==='all'?SUBJECTS:[current];let html='<p class="v95-muted">Recorded answers and lessons are shown separately. These are evidence counts, not a grade or a percentage mastered.</p>';
 for(const subject of subjects){const rows=data.by[subject]||[];let active=rows.filter(x=>x.items||x.independent);if(!active.length&&current==='all')continue;const hint=choose(subject);html+=`<section class="v95-sub"><div class="v95-sub-head"><div><h3>${LABEL[subject]}</h3><small>${active.length} skills with saved activity</small></div><button type="button" data-v95-go="${subject}">Practice ${LABEL[subject]}</button></div>${active.length?'<div class="v95-skill-list">'+active.sort((a,b)=>(b.due>0)-(a.due>0)||(b.missed>0)-(a.missed>0)||b.last-a.last).map(x=>`<article class="v95-skill"><div><strong>${esc(x.label)}</strong><span>${esc(x.status)}</span></div><small>${x.independent} clean · ${x.assisted} assisted · ${x.missed} misses ${x.exposed?'· '+x.exposed+' lessons visited':''}</small></article>`).join('')+'</div>':'<p class="v95-muted">No checked practice recorded yet.</p>'}<p class="v95-next">${hint?'Next useful step: '+esc(hint.label):'Pick a small exercise to start.'}</p></section>`}
 d.innerHTML=html+`<footer class="v95-footnote">${data.historical} compatible older records and ${data.events} newer answers indexed. Older scoring rules are not assumed equivalent. Legacy records are left in place.</footer>`;
 d.querySelectorAll('[data-v95-go]').forEach(b=>b.onclick=()=>goto(b.dataset.v95Go));}
function close(){if(!dialog||!dialog.open)return;dialog.close();previousFocus?.focus?.()}
function openSkills(subject='all'){current=SUBJECTS.includes(subject)?subject:'all';if(!dialog)mount();previousFocus=document.activeElement;dialog.querySelector('#v95Subject').value=current;paint();dialog.showModal();dialog.querySelector('#v95Close').focus()}
function mount(){if(dialog)return;dialog=document.createElement('dialog');dialog.id='v95Skills';dialog.setAttribute('aria-label','Your skills');dialog.innerHTML=`<div class="v95-head"><div><small>STUDY TABLE · YOUR PROGRESS</small><h2>What you’re learning</h2></div><button type="button" id="v95Close" aria-label="Close skills">Close</button></div><div class="v95-content"><label for="v95Subject">Subject</label><select id="v95Subject"><option value="all">All subjects</option>${SUBJECTS.map(s=>`<option value="${s}">${LABEL[s]}</option>`).join('')}</select><div id="v95Content"></div><div class="v95-actions"><button type="button" id="v95OpenBackup">Save or restore progress</button></div></div>`;document.body.append(dialog);dialog.querySelector('#v95Close').onclick=close;dialog.querySelector('#v95Subject').onchange=e=>{current=e.target.value;paint()};dialog.querySelector('#v95OpenBackup').onclick=()=>{close();window.studyTableV90?.openDashboard?.()};dialog.addEventListener('cancel',e=>{e.preventDefault();close()});}
function addButtons(){const v90=document.querySelector('#v90Panel footer');if(v90&&!document.getElementById('v95FromDashboard')){const b=document.createElement('button');b.id='v95FromDashboard';b.className='v95-entry';b.textContent='Explore skills and what to practise next';b.onclick=()=>{window.studyTableV90?.closeDashboard?.();openSkills()};v90.append(b)}
 const choices=document.getElementById('v90SubjectChoices');if(choices&&!document.getElementById('v95LabLink')){const p=document.createElement('div');p.className='v95-lab-links';p.id='v95LabLink';p.innerHTML='<p>Learn by experimenting</p><button type="button" id="v95CalcLab">Calculus labs</button><button type="button" id="v95PhysicsLab">Physics labs</button>';choices.append(p);p.querySelector('#v95CalcLab').onclick=()=>window.studyTableV95Labs?.open('calculus');p.querySelector('#v95PhysicsLab').onclick=()=>window.studyTableV95Labs?.open('physics')}
}
let pending=false;function updateLinks(){if(pending)return;pending=true;queueMicrotask(()=>{pending=false;addButtons()})}
window.studyTableV95={record,refresh,aggregate,choose,openSkills,close,inspect:()=>read(),getSkills:()=>aggregate().skills};
if(document.readyState==='loading')document.addEventListener('DOMContentLoaded',()=>{refresh();mount();addButtons();document.getElementById('v35Portal')?.addEventListener('click',updateLinks)},{once:true});else {refresh();mount();addButtons();document.getElementById('v35Portal')?.addEventListener('click',updateLinks)}
})();

</script>

<script id="v95_concept_labs">
/* Study Table V95 — ten interactive calculus and physics concept labs.
   Experiments teach the model. Separate numerical questions check transfer, not screen-reading. */
(()=>{'use strict';
const S=(id,label,min,max,value,step=1,unit='')=>({id,label,min,max,value,step,unit});
const Q=(prompt,answer,unit,hint,why,mistakes={})=>({prompt,answer,unit,hint,why,mistakes});
const LABS=[
 {id:'slope-lab',subject:'calculus',level:'1 · Start here',title:'What a slope really measures',hook:'A steep hill and a gentle hill can cover the same horizontal distance. What changes?',core:'Slope compares the vertical change to the horizontal change: rise ÷ run.',controls:[S('rise','Vertical rise',1,15,6),S('run','Horizontal run',1,10,3)],visual:'slope',worked:'If you climb 6 m while moving 3 m across, the slope is 6 ÷ 3 = 2. That means 2 m up for every 1 m across.',idea:'Slope is a ratio, not just the height. Doubling both rise and run gives the same slope.',questions:[Q('A road climbs 15 metres over 5 metres horizontally. What is the slope?',3,'','Divide vertical rise by horizontal run.','Slope = 15 ÷ 5 = 3.',{'0.333333':'That is run ÷ rise; slope is rise ÷ run.','20':'Adding distances does not give slope.'}),Q('A line has slope 4. When x increases by 3, how far does y rise?',12,'','Multiply slope by the horizontal change.','Rise = slope × run = 4 × 3 = 12.',{'7':'Slope is a ratio, not a separate amount to add.'})]},
 {id:'derivative-lab',subject:'calculus',level:'2 · A moving world',title:'The speed at one instant',hook:'The speedometer shows one number, even though distance is measured across an interval.',core:'A derivative is the rate of change at one instant. A shorter interval makes average speed approach it.',controls:[S('t','Time',0,6,2,.5,'s'),S('dt','Measured interval',.1,2,1,.1,'s')],visual:'derivative',worked:'If s(t) = t² + 2t, average speed from t = 2 to 3 is (15 − 8) ÷ 1 = 7 m/s. Shrink that interval toward zero and you approach 2t + 2 = 6 m/s at t = 2.',idea:'The instantaneous slope is the limit of secant slopes as the two points get closer.',questions:[Q('For s(t) = t² + 2t, what is the instantaneous velocity at t = 4 (m/s)?',10,'m/s','The derivative is 2t + 2.','v(4) = 2(4) + 2 = 10 m/s.',{'8':'You missed the derivative of the 2t term.'}),Q('For s(t) = t², find the average velocity from t = 3 to t = 4 (m/s).',7,'m/s','Compute s(4) − s(3), then divide by 4 − 3.','(16 − 9) ÷ 1 = 7 m/s.',{'6':'Six is the instantaneous velocity at t = 3, not the average over the full second.'})]},

 {id:'force-lab',subject:'physics',level:'1 · Start here',title:'What makes something accelerate?',hook:'One person pushes a cart right; friction resists it.
