if(new Set(LABS.map(x=>x.id)).size!==LABS.length)throw Error('Repeated lab identifiers');
const $=id=>document.getElementById(id), esc=s=>String(s??'').replace(/[&<>"']/g,c=>({'&':'&amp;','<':'&lt;','>':'&gt;','"':'&quot;',"'":'&#39;'}[c]));
const fmt=n=>Number.isFinite(n)?Number(n.toFixed(2)).toString():'—';
let dlg=null,active=null,subject='calculus',step=0,wrong=false,helped=false,started=0,focusBack=null,answered=false;
function model(L,p){switch(L.visual){
 case 'slope':return {key:'Slope',value:fmt(p.rise/p.run),sub:`${fmt(p.rise)} ÷ ${fmt(p.run)} = ${fmt(p.rise/p.run)} metres up for each metre across`};
 case 'derivative':{const v=2*p.t+2,avg=2*p.t+p.dt+2;return{key:'Instantaneous velocity',value:fmt(v)+' m/s',sub:`Average across ${fmt(p.dt)} s = ${fmt(avg)} m/s. As Δt shrinks, it approaches ${fmt(v)}.`}}
 case 'integral':return {key:'Distance travelled',value:fmt(.5*p.a*p.t*p.t)+' m',sub:`½ × ${fmt(p.t)} s × ${fmt(p.a*p.t)} m/s = ${fmt(.5*p.a*p.t*p.t)} m`};
 case 'chain':return {key:'Rate of output change',value:fmt(2*(p.k*p.x+p.b)*p.k),sub:`2 × (${fmt(p.k*p.x+p.b)}) × ${fmt(p.k)} = ${fmt(2*(p.k*p.x+p.b)*p.k)}`};
 case 'garden':{const length=p.p/2-p.w,area=p.w*length;return {key:'Garden area',value:fmt(area)+' m²',sub:`${fmt(p.w)} × ${fmt(length)} = ${fmt(area)} m². Best possible = ${fmt((p.p/4)**2)} m².`}}
 case 'force':{const F=p.push-p.friction;return{key:'Acceleration',value:fmt(F/p.mass)+' m/s²',sub:`Net force = ${fmt(F)} N, then ${fmt(F)} ÷ ${fmt(p.mass)} kg = ${fmt(F/p.mass)} m/s².`}}
 case 'energy':return {key:'Starting potential energy',value:fmt(p.m*p.g*p.h)+' J',sub:`mgh = ${fmt(p.m)} × ${fmt(p.g)} × ${fmt(p.h)}. Bottom speed ≈ ${fmt(Math.sqrt(2*p.g*p.h))} m/s.`};
 case 'circuit':return {key:'Electric current',value:fmt(p.v/p.r)+' A',sub:`I = ${fmt(p.v)} V ÷ ${fmt(p.r)} Ω = ${fmt(p.v/p.r)} A; power = ${fmt(p.v*p.v/p.r)} W.`};
 case 'fall':return {key:'Speed and distance',value:fmt(p.g*p.t)+' m/s',sub:`Speed = ${fmt(p.g*p.t)} m/s; distance = ${fmt(.5*p.g*p.t*p.t)} m.`};
 case 'momentum':{const total=p.m1+p.m2,imp=p.m1*p.v1+p.m2*p.v2;return {key:'Shared velocity after sticking',value:fmt(imp/total)+' m/s',sub:`Total momentum ${fmt(imp)} kg·m/s ÷ combined mass ${fmt(total)} kg.`}}
 }}
const svgStart=(label)=>`<svg viewBox="0 0 480 240" role="img" aria-label="${esc(label)}" xmlns="http://www.w3.org/2000/svg">`;
const text=(x,y,s,size=13,colour='#385b54')=>`<text x="${x}" y="${y}" font-size="${size}" fill="${colour}" font-family="system-ui, sans-serif">${esc(s)}</text>`;
function plot(L,p){let svg=svgStart(L.title);const axis='<path d="M45 198 H448 M45 198 V20" stroke="#a1b8ad" fill="none" stroke-width="2"/>';
 switch(L.visual){
 case 'slope':{const w=55+p.run*26,h=28+p.rise*9;svg+=axis+`<path d="M76 191 H${76+w} V${191-h}" fill="none" stroke="#d29b54" stroke-width="4" stroke-dasharray="7 5"/><path d="M76 191 L${76+w} ${191-h}" stroke="#256a53" stroke-width="5" stroke-linecap="round"/>`+text(80,217,`Run: ${fmt(p.run)}`)+text(290,53,`Rise: ${fmt(p.rise)}`);break}
 case 'derivative':{const X=t=>45+58*t,Y=t=>196-(t*t+2*t)*3;let path='';for(let i=0;i<=60;i++){const t=i/10;path+=(i?'L':'M')+X(t).toFixed(2)+' '+Y(t).toFixed(2)}const a=p.t,b=p.t+p.dt;svg+=axis+`<path d="${path}" stroke="#287257" stroke-width="3" fill="none"/><line x1="${X(a)}" y1="${Y(a)}" x2="${X(b)}" y2="${Y(b)}" stroke="#c88a44" stroke-width="3"/><circle cx="${X(a)}" cy="${Y(a)}" r="5" fill="#1b6a88"/>`+text(55,18,'Position s(t) = t² + 2t')+text(355,218,'Time →');break}
 case 'integral':{const y=190-18*p.a*p.t;svg+=axis+`<path d="M45 190 L${45+p.t*45} ${Math.max(15,y)} L${45+p.t*45} 190 Z" fill="#b6d7c8" opacity=".8"/><path d="M45 190 L${45+p.t*45} ${Math.max(15,y)}" stroke="#267155" stroke-width="4"/>`+text(65,25,'Area under speed–time graph')+text(353,218,'Time →');break}
 case 'chain':{const x=40+p.x*72,inner=p.k*p.x+p.b;svg+=`<rect x="18" y="55" width="128" height="92" rx="14" fill="#dfebe5" stroke="#a9c7b4"/><rect x="181" y="55" width="128" height="92" rx="14" fill="#eaf0dc" stroke="#afc3a3"/><path d="M146 101 H180 M309 101 H432" stroke="#508f70" stroke-width="4"/>`+text(39,89,`u = ${p.k}x + ${p.b}`,15)+text(214,89,'y = u²',17)+text(24,183,`x = ${p.x}`)+text(185,183,`u = ${inner}`)+text(337,183,`y = ${inner*inner}`);break}
 case 'garden':{const le=p.p/2-p.w,scale=13,x0=140,y0=38;svg+=`<rect x="${x0}" y="${y0}" width="${Math.max(10,p.w*scale)}" height="${Math.max(10,le*scale)}" rx="2" fill="#d4e6cb" stroke="#3e7c51" stroke-width="5"/>`+text(35,218,`Width ${fmt(p.w)} m`)+text(290,218,`Length ${fmt(le)} m`)+text(35,22,'Same perimeter; change the shape');break}
 case 'force':{const right=45+p.push*3.3,left=45+p.friction*3.3;svg+=`<path d="M15 194 H460" stroke="#adc4b9" stroke-width="4"/><rect x="170" y="93" width="140" height="85" rx="9" fill="#d5e8dd" stroke="#357d60" stroke-width="3"/><circle cx="200" cy="186" r="11" fill="#526f63"/><circle cx="280" cy="186" r="11" fill="#526f63"/><path d="M310 116 H${Math.min(460,310+right)} l-13 -8 m13 8 l-13 8" stroke="#248451" stroke-width="4" fill="none"/><path d="M170 146 H${Math.max(10,170-left)} l13 -8 m-13 8 l13 8" stroke="#bc7e48" stroke-width="4" fill="none"/>`+text(195,135,`${p.mass} kg`,20)+text(318,90,`${p.push} N →`)+text(27,124,`← ${p.friction} N`);break}
 case 'energy':{const height=p.h*10+25;svg+=`<path d="M50 190 L210 ${190-height} L410 190 Z" fill="#d2e4d5" stroke="#4e8362" stroke-width="3"/><circle cx="210" cy="${180-height}" r="16" fill="#b97b46"/><line x1="224" y1="${180-height}" x2="430" y2="${180-height}" stroke="#9bafaa" stroke-dasharray="5 5"/>`+text(240,48,`Height ${p.h} m`)+text(45,224,'Potential energy → kinetic energy');break}
 case 'circuit':{svg+=`<path d="M80 48 H388 V188 H80 V48" stroke="#3c7862" stroke-width="5" fill="none"/><rect x="208" y="34" width="86" height="27" fill="#fff4d9" stroke="#ac8349" stroke-width="3"/><line x1="69" y1="107" x2="91" y2="107" stroke="#3c7862" stroke-width="6"/><line x1="60" y1="127" x2="100" y2="127" stroke="#3c7862" stroke-width="3"/>`+text(119,115,`${p.v} V`)+text(217,24,`${p.r} Ω`)+text(158,216,'Closed circuit');break}
 case 'fall':{const dist=.5*p.g*p.t*p.t;svg+=`<path d="M235 18 V214" stroke="#91b5a1" stroke-dasharray="6 5" stroke-width="2"/><circle cx="235" cy="${Math.min(200,35+dist*1.2)}" r="18" fill="#bd8258"/><path d="M315 50 V${Math.min(200,35+dist*1.2)} l-8 -10 m8 10 l8 -10" stroke="#3c8363" stroke-width="3" fill="none"/>`+text(322,108,`v = ${fmt(p.g*p.t)} m/s`)+text(36,34,`t = ${p.t} s`)+text(35,214,`Distance ${fmt(dist)} m`);break}
 case 'momentum':{const speed=(p.m1*p.v1+p.m2*p.v2)/(p.m1+p.m2);svg+=`<path d="M30 190 H450" stroke="#9db3a3" stroke-width="4"/><rect x="110" y="115" width="100" height="60" rx="8" fill="#c8e4dc" stroke="#438478" stroke-width="3"/><rect x="280" y="115" width="100" height="60" rx="8" fill="#e5d5bf" stroke="#b38854" stroke-width="3"/>`+text(127,150,`${p.m1} kg`)+text(300,150,`${p.m2} kg`)+text(111,92,`${p.v1} m/s`)+text(286,92,`${p.v2} m/s`)+text(130,216,`Stuck together → ${fmt(speed)} m/s`);break}
 }
 return svg+'</svg>';}
function createDialog(){if(dlg)return;dlg=document.createElement('dialog');dlg.id='v95LabDlg';dlg.setAttribute('aria-label','Calculus and physics laboratories');dlg.innerHTML='<header class="v95-lab-head"><div><small>STUDY TABLE · INTERACTIVE SCIENCE</small><strong>Understand it by changing it</strong></div><button id="v95LabClose" aria-label="Close labs" type="button">Close</button></header><div id="v95LabBody"></div>';document.body.append(dlg);$('v95LabClose').onclick=close;dlg.addEventListener('cancel',e=>{e.preventDefault();close()});}
function close(){if(dlg?.open){dlg.close();focusBack?.focus?.()}}
function open(sub='calculus',id){createDialog();if(['calculus','physics'].includes(sub))subject=sub;active=LABS.find(x=>x.id===id&&x.subject===subject)||null;step=0;wrong=false;helped=false;answered=false;started=Date.now();focusBack=document.activeElement;render();if(!dlg.open)dlg.showModal();$('v95LabClose').focus();}
function list(){const root=$('v95LabBody');root.innerHTML=`<nav class="v95-lab-tabs" aria-label="Choose subject"><button type="button" data-v95-sub="calculus" ${subject==='calculus'?'aria-current="page"':''}>Calculus</button><button type="button" data-v95-sub="physics" ${subject==='physics'?'aria-current="page"':''}>Physics</button></nav><section class="v95-lab-welcome"><small>EXPLORE • UNDERSTAND • TRY IT</small><h1>${subject==='calculus'?'See the mathematics change':'See the physics happen'}</h1><p>${subject==='calculus'?'Change the numbers and watch the graph. Then solve a different example yourself.':'Experiment with a physical model. Understand which forces or quantities matter, then work through a fresh question.'}</p></section><div class="v95-lab-grid">${LABS.filter(x=>x.subject===subject).map((x,i)=>`<button type="button" class="v95-lab-tile" data-v95-id="${x.id}"><small>${esc(x.level)}</small><strong>${esc(x.title)}</strong><span>${esc(x.hook)}</span><b>Open experiment</b></button>`).join('')}</div>${window.studyTableV96?.cards(subject)||''}<p class="v95-lab-note">The models deliberately simplify reality and say so when friction or resistance is ignored. Progress from the independent questions appears in your skill map.</p>`;
 root.querySelectorAll('[data-v96-route]').forEach(b=>b.onclick=()=>window.studyTableV96?.open(b.dataset.v96Route,subject));root.querySelectorAll('[data-v95-sub]').forEach(b=>b.onclick=()=>{subject=b.dataset.v95Sub;render()});root.querySelectorAll('[data-v95-id]').forEach(b=>b.onclick=()=>{active=LABS.find(x=>x.id===b.dataset.v95Id);step=0;wrong=false;helped=false;answered=false;started=Date.now();render()});}
function params(L){return Object.fromEntries(L.controls.map(x=>[x.id,+$("v95s_"+x.id).value]))}
function visual(){const root=$('v95LabVisual');if(!root||!active)return;const p=params(active),m=model(active,p);root.innerHTML=`<div class="v95-lab-diagram">${plot(active,p)}</div><div class="v95-lab-readout"><small>${esc(m.key)}</small><strong>${esc(m.value)}</strong><p>${esc(m.sub)}</p></div>`;active.controls.forEach(c=>{$('v95v_'+c.id).textContent=fmt(p[c.id])+' '+c.unit})}
function feedback(s,tone){const box=$('v95LabFeedback');box.innerHTML=s;box.dataset.tone=tone||''}
function check(){if(!active||answered)return;const L=active,q=L.questions[step],txt=$('v95LabAnswer').value.trim();if(!txt){feedback('Enter your answer first. You can include the unit if you want.','help');return}let n=Number(txt.replace(',','.').replace(/\s*[a-zA-Z²Ω/·]+.*$/u,'').trim());if(!Number.isFinite(n)){feedback('Use a number, such as 2, −1 or 3.5.','help');return}
 const tol=Math.max(.00001,Math.abs(q.answer)*.00001);if(Math.abs(n-q.answer)<=tol){const status=wrong||helped?'assisted':'independent';window.studyTableV95?.record({subject:L.subject,skill:L.id,item:'lab:'+L.id+':q'+step,status});answered=true;feedback(`<strong>${status==='independent'?'Correct, without help.':'That is correct.'}</strong><p>${esc(q.why)}</p><small>${status==='independent'?'Recorded as an independent answer.':'Recorded as practice with help or corrections.'}</small><button type="button" id="v95LabNext">${step===1?'Finish this experiment':'Try the next question'}</button>`,'correct');$('v95LabNext').onclick=()=>{if(step===1)done();else{step++;wrong=false;helped=false;answered=false;render()}};return}
 if(!wrong)window.studyTableV95?.record({subject:L.subject,skill:L.id,item:'lab:'+L.id+':q'+step,status:'missed'});wrong=true;const custom=q.mistakes[String(n)]||'Check the quantities, direction, and units before trying again.';feedback(`<strong>Not quite.</strong><p>${esc(custom)}</p><p>Try once more, or use a clue.</p>`,'wrong');$('v95LabAnswer').focus();}
function help(reveal){if(!active||answered)return;helped=true;const q=active.questions[step];if(reveal){window.studyTableV95?.record({subject:active.subject,skill:active.id,item:'lab:'+active.id+':q'+step,status:'revealed'});answered=true;feedback(`<strong>Here’s the worked answer: ${fmt(q.answer)} ${esc(q.unit)}</strong><p>${esc(q.why)}</p><p>This counts as studying the solution, not solving it independently.</p><button id="v95LabNext" type="button">${step===1?'Finish this experiment':'Next question'}</button>`,'help');$('v95LabNext').onclick=()=>{if(step===1)done();else{step++;wrong=false;helped=false;answered=false;render()}}}else feedback(`<strong>A clue</strong><p>${esc(q.hint)}</p><small>Using a clue makes this a guided attempt.</small>`,'help')}
function done(){const L=active;const a=window.studyTableV95?.aggregate().skills.find(x=>x.id===L.subject+'.'+L.id);$('v95LabBody').innerHTML=`<div class="v95-lab-finish"><small>EXPERIMENT COMPLETE</small><h2>${esc(L.title)}</h2><p>Nice work going from a model to an answer. You can see any hints and corrections in the worked explanations when you repeat this later.</p><p>${a?`${a.independent} independently checked answers and ${a.missed} missed attempts recorded for this idea.`:''}</p><div class="v95-lab-actions"><button id="v95Another" type="button">Choose another experiment</button><button id="v95Repeat" type="button">Try this one again</button><button id="v95MySkills" type="button">See my skills</button></div></div>`;$('v95Another').onclick=()=>{active=null;render()};$('v95Repeat').onclick=()=>{step=0;wrong=false;helped=false;answered=false;render()};$('v95MySkills').onclick=()=>{close();window.studyTableV95?.openSkills(L.subject)};}
function render(){if(!active){list();return}const L=active,q=L.questions[step],c=L.controls,root=$('v95LabBody');root.innerHTML=`<div class="v95-lab-breadcrumb"><button id="v95LabAll" type="button">All experiments</button><span>${esc(L.subject==='calculus'?'Calculus':'Physics')} · ${esc(L.level)}</span></div><div class="v95-lab-story"><small>${esc(L.level)}</small><h1>${esc(L.title)}</h1><p>${esc(L.hook)}</p></div><div class="v95-lab-col"><section class="v95-lab-explore"><h2>1. Change the experiment</h2><p>${esc(L.core)}</p><div class="v95-lab-sliders">${c.map(x=>`<label for="v95s_${x.id}">${esc(x.label)} <output id="v95v_${x.id}">${x.value} ${esc(x.unit)}</output><input id="v95s_${x.id}" type="range" min="${x.min}" max="${x.max}" step="${x.step}" value="${x.value}"></label>`).join('')}</div><div id="v95LabVisual" aria-live="off"></div></section><div class="v95-lab-teaching"><section class="v95-lab-worked"><small>SEE ONE WORKED EXAMPLE</small><p>${esc(L.worked)}</p></section><section class="v95-lab-understanding"><small>THE IDEA TO REMEMBER</small><p>${esc(L.idea)}</p></section></div></div><section class="v95-lab-quiz"><div class="v95-lab-question-head"><small>2. YOUR TURN</small><span>Question ${step+1} of 2</span></div><h2>${esc(q.prompt)}</h2><p class="v95-lab-muted">These are different numbers from the example above. Try answering before using a clue.</p><form id="v95LabForm"><label for="v95LabAnswer">Your answer ${q.unit?`(${esc(q.unit)})`:''}</label><div class="v95-lab-answer-row"><input type="text" inputmode="decimal" autocomplete="off" id="v95LabAnswer" placeholder="Write a number"><button type="submit">Check my answer</button></div></form><div class="v95-lab-actions"><button type="button" id="v95LabHint">A small clue</button><button type="button" id="v95LabReveal">Show the worked answer</button></div><div id="v95LabFeedback" class="v95-lab-feedback" role="status" aria-live="polite"></div></section>`;
 $('v95LabAll').onclick=()=>{active=null;render()};root.querySelectorAll('input[type=range]').forEach(x=>x.oninput=visual);$('v95LabForm').onsubmit=e=>{e.preventDefault();check()};$('v95LabHint').onclick=()=>help(false);$('v95LabReveal').onclick=()=>help(true);visual();}
window.studyTableV95Labs={open,close,LABS:LABS.map(x=>({id:x.id,subject:x.subject,title:x.title,questions:x.questions.length})),inspect:()=>({subject,id:active?.id,step,wrong,helped,answered}),models:LABS.map(L=>({id:L.id,default:model(L,Object.fromEntries(L.controls.map(c=>[c.id,c.value])))}))};
})();

</script>

<script id="v97-teacher-script">

Soy supplies I am for this identification. Estudiante can describe a student of any gender.","Estoy en la biblioteca. Estoy en introduces your location. Biblioteca means library.","Tengo una pregunta. Una pregunta is a question. Tengo supplies I have.","Soy estudiante. Estoy en la biblioteca. Tengo una pregunta. Pause between the sentences. Clear short sentences are enough to begin the exchange."],"rule":"Use soy, estoy and tengo in three useful first-person sentence patterns.","trap":"This sentence is trying to locate a person. In this construction Spanish uses estar, so the I-form is estoy. The fact that the library is a fixed building does not decide the verb.","questions":[{"prompt":"You want to say you are at home. Which sentence fits?","choices":["Soy en casa.","Estoy en casa.","Tengo en casa."],"correct":1,"feedback":["Soy identifies you in our examples; use estoy for your location.","Estoy en casa gives your location: I am at home.","Tengo means I have; this does not complete that thought."],"hint":"Ask which pattern gives a person’s location.","nudge":"Is this an identity, a location, or something you have?"},{"prompt":"Why does soy estudiante use soy even though you might graduate later?","choices":["Soy only describes facts that last forever.","Estudiante tells the listener your location.","The sentence identifies you as a student."],"correct":2,"feedback":["Permanence is not a reliable rule for this choice.","Estudiante means student; it does not name a location.","This is an identification, so soy fits even though your status can change."],"hint":"Describe the job of the whole sentence.","nudge":"What does this sentence tell the other person about you?"}],"why":"English gives us ‘I am’ in both ‘I am a student’ and ‘I am in the library.’ Spanish makes a different choice: soy estudiante identifies you, while estoy en la biblioteca locates you. Learn these as small, useful patterns before trying to predict every use of ser and estar. The distinction is not simply permanent versus temporary: a student can graduate, but the identification here still uses soy. For ‘I have a question,’ use tengo una pregunta. Tengo already tells the listener that you mean I have; yo is usually unnecessary unless you want emphasis or contrast. Listen to each sentence as a whole, then change one part. Estoy en casa means I am at home. Tengo una duda means I have a doubt or question. Start with three phrases: quiero agua, tengo hambre, puedo pagar.","steps":["Start with the person: yo means I; tú means you.","Use quiero for “I want,” tengo for “I have,” and puedo for “I can.” These are not word-for-word English endings.","Add a small useful phrase: Quiero agua. Tengo hambre. Puedo pagar.","Say your own sentence aloud, swapping one noun. Use it before worrying about perfection."],"rule":"Quiero + noun = I want something. Quiero already says that I am the one who wants something. That is why you will often hear the sentence without yo, the separate word for I. Comer tells us what I want to do. Keep that part unchanged and you can swap the activity: Quiero beber, I want to drink. Quiero salir, I want to go out. Say one pair slowly enough to hear what stays and what changes. The hunger sentence works differently. Spanish says Tengo hambre, literally I have hunger. Knowing the English sentence I am hungry will not let you translate this one word by word. const V102_NUMBERS={
'calculus-1':{q:'A cyclist moves from 8 m to 44 m between t=1 s and t=7 s. What is the average velocity?',answer:6,unit:'m/s',why:'Use changes: (44−8)/(7−1)=36/6=6 m/s.'},
'calculus-2':{q:'Position is s(t)=3t² metres. What is instantaneous velocity at t=4 s?',answer:24,unit:'m/s',why:'Differentiate first: v(t)=6t, so v(4)=24 m/s.'},
'calculus-3':{q:'For y=(2x+3)², what is dy/dx at x=2?',answer:28,unit:'(numerical derivative)',why:'The chain rule gives y′=2(2x+3)×2. At x=2, this is 28.'},
'calculus-4':{q:'Velocity increases linearly from 0 to 8 m/s over 6 s. What is the displacement?',answer:24,unit:'m',why:'The area under velocity versus time is a triangle: ½×6×8=24 m.'},
'calculus-5':{q:'For f(x)=−x²+14x, at what x does the maximum occur?',answer:7,unit:'(x value)',why:'f′=−2x+14=0 gives x=7. The negative second derivative confirms a maximum.'},
'physics-1':{q:'A 4 kg cart is pushed 22 N right while friction is 6 N left. What is its rightward acceleration?',answer:4,unit:'m/s²',why:'Net force is 22−6=16 N right. Divide by 4 kg to get 4 m/s² right.'},
'physics-2':{q:'A sled starts from rest and drops 45 m with negligible friction. Use g=10 m/s². What is its final speed?',answer:30,unit:'m/s',why:'v=√(2gh)=√(2×10×45)=√900=30 m/s.'},
'physics-3':{q:'An ideal 18 V source is connected across a 6 Ω resistor. What current flows?',answer:3,unit:'A',why:'Ohm’s law gives I=V/R=18/6=3 A.'},
'physics-4':{q:'An object is dropped from rest. With g=10 m/s² and no air resistance, how far does it fall in 2 s?',answer:20,unit:'m',why:'Distance=½gt²=½×10×4=20 m. Speed at that instant is a different quantity.'},
'physics-5':{q:'A 2 kg cart moving right at 6 m/s sticks to a 4 kg cart at rest. With negligible external impulse, what is the shared rightward velocity?',answer:2,unit:'m/s',why:'Momentum is 2×6=12 kg·m/s right. Shared mass is 6 kg, so velocity is 2 m/s right.'},
'business-1':{q:'Your stall sells a product for $45; variable cost is $27 and fixed costs are $900. How many sales break even?',answer:50,unit:'sales',why:'Contribution is $18 per sale, so break-even is 900/18=50 sales, under this simplified cost model.'},
'economics-1':{q:'You already paid a nonrefundable $15 entry fee. Going means giving up your next-best $40 shift. What is the foregone pay if you go?',answer:40,unit:'dollars',why:'Foregone pay is $40. The $15 already paid is sunk; tonight’s decision cannot recover it.'}
};
function v102Number(box,L){const q=V102_NUMBERS[L.id];if(!q)return;const lab=document.createElement('section');lab.className='v102-number';lab.innerHTML=`<small>OPTIONAL NUMBER CHALLENGE · AUTOMATIC FEEDBACK</small><h3>A fresh set of numbers</h3><p>${escape(q.q)}</p><label for="v102Number">Enter just the number; unit: ${escape(q.unit)}</label><input id="v102Number" type="number" step="any" inputmode="decimal"><button type="button" data-number-check>Check this number</button><p data-number-feedback role="status"></p>`;const before=box.querySelector('[data-v102-model]');before.before(lab);let attempts=0;lab.querySelector('button').onclick=()=>{const input=lab.querySelector('input'),feedback=lab.querySelector('[data-number-feedback]');if(!input.value.trim()||!Number.isFinite(input.valueAsNumber)){feedback.textContent='Enter a finite number before checking.';input.focus();return}const correct=Math.abs(input.valueAsNumber-q.answer)<1e-6;const afterModel=!box.querySelector('[data-v102-model]').hidden;attempts++;const record=v102Record(L.id),prior=record.numberCheck;const observed={firstTryCorrect:prior?.firstTryCorrect??(attempts===1&&correct),afterModel:prior?.afterModel??afterModel,lastCorrect:correct,at:Date.now(),attempts:(prior?.attempts||0)+1};record.numberCheck=observed;const saved=v102Write(L.id,record);feedback.textContent=(correct?'That number is correct. ':'Not quite. ')+q.why+(attempts>1?' This is a retry after feedback.':'')+(saved?'':' Result could not be saved.');};}

// Read the same event schedule that playback uses. No microphone or performance grading.
function v127PianoRows(pattern){
 const epsilon=1e-7,step=pattern.subdivide===2?.5:1,meter=pattern.meter||4;
 const times=new Set([pattern.beats]);
 for(let t=0;t<pattern.beats;t+=step)times.add(t);
 for(const event of pattern.events){times.add(event.at);times.add(event.at+event.beats)}
 return [...times].filter(t=>t<=pattern.beats).sort((a,b)=>a-b).map(at=>{
  const start=pattern.events.filter(e=>Math.abs(e.at-at)<epsilon).map(e=>e.note);
  const keep=pattern.events.filter(e=>e.at<at-epsilon&&e.at+e.beats>at+epsilon).map(e=>e.note);
  const release=pattern.events.filter(e=>Math.abs(e.at+e.beats-at)<epsilon&&!start.includes(e.note)).map(e=>e.note);
  const beat=at%meter,whole=Math.floor(beat),fraction=beat-whole;
  const count=at===pattern.beats?'End':('Bar '+(Math.floor(at/meter)+1)+', '+(whole+1)+(fraction===.5?' and':fraction?' + '+fraction:''));
  const action=[release.length?'Release '+release.join(', ')+'.':'',start.length?'Press '+start.join(' + ')+'.':'',keep.length?'Keep '+keep.join(' + ')+' down.':'',!start.length&&!keep.length&&at<pattern.beats?'Silence; keep counting.':''].filter(Boolean).join(' ');
  return {at,count,action};
 });
}
function v127PianoTiming(host,pattern){
 const target=host.querySelector('[data-piano-timing]');if(!target)return;
 target.innerHTML='<details'+(pattern.beats<=4?' open':'')+'><summary>Follow the timing: '+escape(pattern.label)+'</summary><p>Press starts a note. Keep means leave that key down. Release ends it. The count continues through silence. C4 is middle C; the numbers in note names locate keys, not fingers.</p><table><caption>'+escape(pattern.label)+' · '+pattern.beats+' beats. Each numbered beat is one pulse; “and” is halfway to the next pulse.</caption><thead><tr><th scope="col">Count</th><th scope="col">What the fingers do</th></tr></thead><tbody>'+v127PianoRows(pattern).map(r=>'<tr data-piano-time="'+r.at+'"><th scope="row">'+escape(r.count)+'</th><td>'+escape(r.action)+'</td></tr>').join('')+'</tbody></table></details>';
}
function v127PianoMark(host,at){
 host.querySelectorAll('[data-piano-time]').forEach(row=>{if(Number(row.dataset.pianoTime)===at)row.setAttribute('aria-current','step');else row.removeAttribute('aria-current')});
}

let v109Audio=null,v109Generation=0,v109Timers=new Set(),v109Oscillators=new Set();
function v109StopMedia(){v109Generation++;for(const t of v109Timers)clearTimeout(t);v109Timers.clear();for(const o of v109Oscillators){try{o.stop()}catch{}}v109Oscillators.clear();window.speechSynthesis?.cancel?.();document.querySelectorAll('[data-piano-time][aria-current]').forEach(e=>e.removeAttribute('aria-current'));document.querySelectorAll('.v109-demo .playing,.v109-demo .speaking,.variation-speaking').forEach(e=>e.classList.remove('playing','speaking','variation-speaking'))}
function v109Timer(fn,ms){const timer=setTimeout(()=>{v109Timers.delete(timer);fn()},ms);v109Timers.add(timer);return timer}

function v109Spanish(host,d){
 const viewBody=view=>!view?'':'<dl class="sentence-parts">'+view.parts.map(([part,job])=>'<dt lang="es">'+escape(part)+'</dt><dd>'+escape(job)+'</dd>').join('')+'</dl><figure class="viewpoint-sketch"><svg viewBox="0 0 420 100" role="img" aria-label="'+(view.picture==='whole'?'The activity is presented as a whole past event.':'The reported event sits within an activity already in progress.')+'"><path d="M35 45H385" stroke="currentColor" stroke-width="4"'+(view.picture==='inside'?' stroke-dasharray="7 5"':'')+'/>'+(view.picture==='whole'?'<path d="M35 30V60M385 30V60" stroke="currentColor" stroke-width="3"/><text x="100" y="84">Whole past event</text>':'<circle cx="235" cy="45" r="7" fill="var(--study-warm)"/><text x="35" y="24">Activity in progress</text><text x="195" y="84">Reported event</text>')+'</svg></figure><p>'+escape(view.meaning)+'</p>';
 host.innerHTML='<h3>'+escape(d.title||'Hear the exchange')+'</h3><p>'+escape(d.intro||'Read the Spanish with the English underneath. You can hear the whole exchange or replay one line.')+'</p>'+(d.views?'<label for="v113PastView">Choose the story you want to tell</label><select id="v113PastView">'+d.views.map((v,i)=>'<option value="'+i+'">'+escape(v.label)+'</option>').join('')+'</select>':'')+'<div class="v109-controls"><button type="button" data-conversation=".85">'+escape(d.playLabel||'Hear the exchange')+'</button><button type="button" data-conversation=".65">Hear it slowly</button><button type="button" data-stop>Stop</button></div><ol class="v109-dialogue">'+d.lines.map((line,i)=>'<li data-line="'+i+'"><span class="v109-speaker">'+escape(line[0])+'</span><p lang="es">'+escape(line[1])+'</p><p class="v109-translation">'+escape(line[2])+'</p>'+viewBody(d.views?.[i])+'<button type="button" data-hear-line="'+i+'" aria-label="Hear line '+(i+1)+'">Hear this line</button></li>').join('')+'</ol><p id="v109SpeechStatus" role="status">Audio uses a Spanish voice supplied by your device.</p>';
 const showLine=i=>{if(!d.views)return;host.querySelectorAll('[data-line]').forEach(el=>{el.hidden=Number(el.dataset.line)!==i;el.style.display=el.hidden?'none':''});$('v113PastView').value=String(i)};if(d.views){showLine(0);$('v113PastView').onchange=()=>{v109StopMedia();showLine(Number($('v113PastView').value));$('v109SpeechStatus').textContent='Read the sentence and its parts, or use Hear this line.'}};
 const hear=(indices,rate)=>{v109StopMedia();const generation=v109Generation,status=$('v109SpeechStatus'),synth=window.speechSynthesis,Utterance=window.SpeechSynthesisUtterance;
  if(!synth||!Utterance){status.textContent='Audio is unavailable here. The complete exchange and translations are written above.';return}
  const voices=synth.getVoices().filter(v=>/^es(?:-|$)/i.test(v.lang));if(!voices.length){status.textContent='No Spanish voice is available on this device. You can read the complete exchange and translations above.';return}
  const voice=voices.find(v=>/^es-MX/i.test(v.lang))||voices[0];let pos=0;
  const next=()=>{if(generation!==v109Generation||!host.isConnected)return;host.querySelectorAll('.speaking').forEach(e=>e.classList.remove('speaking'));if(pos===indices.length){status.textContent='Finished. Replay any line you want to hear again.';return}const i=indices[pos++],u=new Utterance(d.lines[i][1]);showLine(i);u.voice=voice;u.lang=voice.lang;u.rate=rate;host.querySelector('[data-line="'+i+'"]').classList.add('speaking');status.textContent=d.lines[i][0]+': '+d.lines[i][1];u.onend=next;u.onerror=()=>{if(generation===v109Generation){host.querySelectorAll('.speaking').forEach(e=>e.classList.remove('speaking'));status.textContent='The voice could not play. Follow the written exchange above.'}};synth.speak(u)};next();
 };
 host.querySelectorAll('[data-conversation]').forEach(b=>b.onclick=()=>hear(d.lines.map((_,i)=>i),Number(b.dataset.conversation)));host.querySelectorAll('[data-hear-line]').forEach(b=>b.onclick=()=>hear([Number(b.dataset.hearLine)],.75));host.querySelector('[data-stop]').onclick=()=>{v109StopMedia();$('v109SpeechStatus').textContent='Stopped.'};
}
function v109Keyboard(notes){const white=notes.filter(n=>!n.includes('#')),black=notes.filter(n=>n.includes('#'));
 return '<div class="v109-keyboard-scroll" role="region" aria-label="Scrollable piano keyboard" tabindex="0"><div class="v109-keys" style="--v126-keyboard-width:'+white.length*48+'px" aria-label="Playable piano demonstration">'+white.map(n=>'<button type="button" data-v109-note="'+n+'" aria-label="Play '+n+'">'+n+'</button>').join('')+black.map(n=>{const previous=white.indexOf(n.replace('#',''));return '<button class="v109-black" style="left:'+((previous+.72)/white.length*100)+'%;width:'+(58/white.length)+'%" type="button" data-v109-note="'+n+'" aria-label="Play '+n.replace('#',' sharp ')+'"></button>'}).join('')+'</div></div>';
}
function v109RevealNote(host,note){const box=host.querySelector('.v109-keyboard-scroll'),key=host.querySelector('[data-v109-note="'+note+'"]');if(box&&key)box.scrollLeft=Math.max(0,key.offsetLeft-box.clientWidth/3)}
function v109Piano(host,d){
 const tempos=[...new Set([40,...(d.tempos||[60,80,120])])].sort((a,b)=>a-b),defaultTempo=d.defaultTempo||60;
 host.innerHTML='<h3>'+escape(d.title)+'</h3><p>'+escape(d.intro)+'</p>'+v109Keyboard(d.keys)+'<label class="v107-tempo" for="v109Tempo">Tempo <select id="v109Tempo">'+tempos.map(n=>'<option value="'+n+'"'+(n===defaultTempo?' selected':'')+'>'+n+' beats/min</option>').join('')+'</select></label><div class="piano-coach-options"><label>Lead-in <select data-piano-lead><option value="auto">Lesson setting</option><option value="bar">Count one bar first</option><option value="none">Start directly</option></select></label><label>Beat clicks <select data-piano-pulse><option value="auto">Lesson setting</option><option value="on">On</option><option value="off">Off</option></select></label></div><div class="v109-controls">'+d.patterns.map((p,i)=>'<button type="button" data-pattern="'+i+'">'+escape(p.label)+'</button>').join('')+'<button type="button" data-stop>Stop</button></div><p id="v109PianoStatus" role="status">'+escape(d.count)+'</p><div class="piano-coach-timing" data-piano-timing></div>';
 v127PianoTiming(host,d.patterns[0]);
 v109RevealNote(host,d.patterns[0]?.events[0]?.note);
 const play=async (pattern,showTiming=true)=>{
  if(showTiming)v127PianoTiming(host,pattern);
  v109RevealNote(host,pattern.events[0]?.note);v109StopMedia();const generation=v109Generation,status=$('v109PianoStatus'),Context=window.AudioContext||window.webkitAudioContext;
  if(!Context){status.textContent='Audio is unavailable here. The notes and timing are written in the solution below.';return}
  const beat=60/Number($('v109Tempo').value),meter=pattern.meter||4,leadChoice=host.querySelector('[data-piano-lead]').value,pulseChoice=host.querySelector('[data-piano-pulse]').value,
   countIn=showTiming?(leadChoice==='bar'?meter:leadChoice==='none'?0:pattern.countIn??d.countIn??0):0,
   pulse=showTiming?(pulseChoice==='on'?true:pulseChoice==='off'?false:pattern.metronome??d.metronome??false):false;
  try{
   v109Audio=v109Audio||new Context();await v109Audio.resume();if(generation!==v109Generation||!host.isConnected)return;
   const lead=v109Audio.currentTime+.04,base=lead+countIn*beat,counts=new Map();
   const mark=(note,change)=>{counts.set(note,(counts.get(note)||0)+change);host.querySelectorAll('[data-v109-note]').forEach(e=>e.classList.toggle('playing',(counts.get(e.dataset.v109Note)||0)>0))};
   if(countIn||pulse)for(let i=0;i<countIn+pattern.beats;i++){
    if(i>=countIn&&!pulse)continue;const osc=v109Audio.createOscillator(),gain=v109Audio.createGain(),start=lead+i*beat;osc.frequency.value=(i<countIn?i:i-countIn)%meter===0?880:660;gain.gain.setValueAtTime(.0001,start);gain.gain.exponentialRampToValueAtTime(.022,start+.008);gain.gain.exponentialRampToValueAtTime(.0001,start+.065);osc.connect(gain);gain.connect(v109Audio.destination);v109Oscillators.add(osc);osc.onended=()=>{v109Oscillators.delete(osc);osc.disconnect();gain.disconnect()};osc.start(start);osc.stop(start+.07);
    if(i<countIn)v109Timer(()=>{if(generation===v109Generation)status.textContent='Count in: '+(i+1)+' of '+countIn},40+i*beat*1000);
   }
   for(const event of pattern.events){
    const match=/^([A-G])(#?)(\d)$/.exec(event.note),semitones={C:0,D:2,E:4,F:5,G:7,A:9,B:11},midi=12*(Number(match[3])+1)+semitones[match[1]]+(match[2]?1:0),osc=v109Audio.createOscillator(),gain=v109Audio.createGain(),start=base+event.at*beat,duration=event.beats*beat;
    const peak=Number.isFinite(event.level)?Math.max(.015,Math.min(.12,event.level)):.055;osc.frequency.value=440*2**((midi-69)/12);gain.gain.setValueAtTime(.0001,start);gain.gain.exponentialRampToValueAtTime(peak,start+.015);gain.gain.exponentialRampToValueAtTime(.0001,start+duration*.94);osc.connect(gain);gain.connect(v109Audio.destination);v109Oscillators.add(osc);osc.onended=()=>{v109Oscillators.delete(osc);osc.disconnect();gain.disconnect()};osc.start(start);osc.stop(start+duration*.97);
    v109Timer(()=>{if(generation===v109Generation)mark(event.note,1)},40+(countIn+event.at)*beat*1000);v109Timer(()=>{if(generation===v109Generation)mark(event.note,-1)},40+(countIn+event.at+event.beats)*beat*1000);
   }
   if(showTiming)for(const row of v127PianoRows(pattern))v109Timer(()=>{if(generation===v109Generation)v127PianoMark(host,row.at)},40+(countIn+row.at)*beat*1000);
   const divisions=pattern.subdivide===2?2:1;for(let i=0;i<pattern.beats*divisions;i++)v109Timer(()=>{if(generation===v109Generation)status.textContent=pattern.label+(divisions===2?' · count '+(Math.floor(i/2)+1)+(i%2?' and':''):pattern.meter?' · count '+(i%pattern.meter+1)+' · group '+(Math.floor(i/pattern.meter)+1):' · beat '+(i+1)+' of '+pattern.beats)},40+(countIn+i/divisions)*beat*1000);
   v109Timer(()=>{if(generation===v109Generation){v109StopMedia();status.textContent='Finished: '+pattern.description}},60+(countIn+pattern.beats)*beat*1000);
  }catch{v109StopMedia();status.textContent='The sound could not play. The written notes and count are below.'}
 };
 host.querySelectorAll('[data-pattern]').forEach(b=>b.onclick=()=>play(d.patterns[Number(b.dataset.pattern)]));host.querySelectorAll('[data-v109-note]').forEach(b=>b.onclick=()=>play({label:b.dataset.v109Note,beats:1,countIn:0,metronome:false,events:[{note:b.dataset.v109Note,at:0,beats:1}],description:b.dataset.v109Note},false));host.querySelector('[data-stop]').onclick=()=>{v109StopMedia();$('v109PianoStatus').textContent='Stopped.'};host.querySelectorAll('.piano-coach-options select').forEach(select=>select.onchange=()=>{v109StopMedia();$('v109PianoStatus').textContent='Playback setting changed. Choose a part to hear it again.'});$('v109Tempo').onchange=()=>{v109StopMedia();$('v109PianoStatus').textContent='Press a playback button to hear the new tempo.'};
}
const V109_PIECES={wK:['♔','white king'],wQ:['♕','white queen'],wR:['♖','white rook'],wB:['♗','white bishop'],wN:['♘','white knight'],wP:['♙','white pawn'],bK:['♚','black king'],bQ:['♛','black queen'],bR:['♜','black rook'],bB:['♝','black bishop'],bN:['♞','black knight'],bP:['♟','black pawn']};
function v109DrawBoard(board,pieces,marks,onSquare){const old=board.contains(document.activeElement)?document.activeElement.dataset.square:null;board.innerHTML='';for(let rank=8;rank>=1;rank--)for(let file=0;file<8;file++){const square='abcdefgh'[file]+rank,b=document.createElement('button'),piece=V109_PIECES[pieces[square]];b.type='button';b.dataset.square=square;b.className=(rank+file)%2?'dark':'light';if(marks.includes(square))b.classList.add('marked');b.setAttribute('aria-label',square+', '+(piece?piece[1]:'empty'));b.tabIndex=square===(old||'d4')?0:-1;const glyph=document.createElement('span');glyph.textContent=piece?.[0]||'';const label=document.createElement('small');label.textContent=square;b.append(glyph,label);b.onclick=()=>onSquare(square);b.onkeydown=e=>{const delta={ArrowLeft:[-1,0],ArrowRight:[1,0],ArrowUp:[0,1],ArrowDown:[0,-1]}[e.key];if(!delta)return;e.preventDefault();const x=file+delta[0],y=rank+delta[1];if(x<0||x>7||y<1||y>8)return;board.querySelectorAll('button').forEach(el=>el.tabIndex=-1);const target=board.querySelector('[data-square="'+'abcdefgh'[x]+y+'"]');target.tabIndex=0;target.focus()};board.append(b)}if(old)board.querySelector('[data-square="'+old+'"]')?.focus()}
function v109Movement(host,d={}){let piece='R',blocked=false;
 host.innerHTML='<h3>Try the movement shapes</h3><p>Choose a piece, then a square. These diagrams show movement and blockers. A complete game also requires every move to keep your king safe.</p><label for="v109Piece">Piece</label> <select id="v109Piece"><option value="R">Rook</option><option value="B">Bishop</option><option value="K">King</option><option value="N">Knight</option>'+(d.complete?'<option value="Q">Queen</option><option value="PW">White pawn</option><option value="PB">Black pawn</option>':'')+'</select><button type="button" id="v113PawnBlock" hidden>Block the forward square</button><div class="v109-board" role="group" aria-label="Piece movement from d4"></div><p id="v109ChessStatus" role="status"></p>';
 const draw=()=>{const pawn=piece==='PW'||piece==='PB',white=piece!=='PB',rank=pawn?(white?2:7):4,from='d'+rank,glyph=pawn?(white?'wP':'bP'):'w'+piece,enemy=white?'b':'w',nextRank=rank+(white?1:-1),pieces={[from]:glyph};
  if(pawn){pieces['c'+nextRank]=enemy+'N';pieces['e'+nextRank]=enemy+'N';if(blocked)pieces['d'+nextRank]=enemy+'R'}
  const reachable=s=>{const x=Math.abs(s.charCodeAt(0)-100),dy=Number(s[1])-rank,y=Math.abs(dy);if(x+y===0)return false;if(pawn){const forward=dy*(white?1:-1);return x===1&&forward===1&&!!pieces[s]||x===0&&!blocked&&(forward===1||forward===2)}return piece==='R'?(x===0||y===0):piece==='B'?x===y:piece==='Q'?(x===0||y===0||x===y):piece==='K'?Math.max(x,y)===1:x*y===2};
  const marks=[];for(let x=0;x<8;x++)for(let y=1;y<=8;y++){const s='abcdefgh'[x]+y;if(reachable(s))marks.push(s)}const board=host.querySelector('.v109-board');board.setAttribute('aria-label','Piece movement from '+from);
  v109DrawBoard(board,pieces,marks,s=>{const capture=pawn&&pieces[s]&&s[0]!=='d';$('v109ChessStatus').textContent=s===from?'The piece begins on '+from+'. Choose a marked destination.':reachable(s)?from+' → '+s+(capture?' captures an enemy piece diagonally.':pawn?' moves forward into an empty square.':' matches the '+V109_PIECES[glyph][1].replace('white ','')+' movement on this empty diagram.'):pawn&&s==='d'+nextRank&&blocked?'A pawn cannot capture straight ahead or jump this blocker. Its diagonal captures remain available.':from+' → '+s+' does not match the movement available in this diagram.'});
  const button=$('v113PawnBlock');button.hidden=!pawn;button.textContent=blocked?'Clear the forward square':'Block the forward square';button.setAttribute('aria-pressed',String(blocked));$('v109ChessStatus').textContent=pawn?marks.length+' destinations from '+from+': '+(blocked?'only the two diagonal captures; the forward square is blocked.':'one or two squares forward into empty squares, or a diagonal capture of a knight.') :marks.length+' destinations match this movement from '+from+' on an empty diagram.';
 };$('v109Piece').onchange=e=>{piece=e.target.value;blocked=false;draw()};$('v113PawnBlock').onclick=()=>{blocked=!blocked;draw()};draw();
}
function v109Chess(host,d){let line=0,pos=0,selected=null;
 host.innerHTML='<h3>Follow the moves on the board</h3><p>'+escape(d.intro||'This board follows the worked lines below. Use Show next move, or select the named piece and its destination. The arrow keys move keyboard focus around the board.')+'</p>'+(d.lines.length>1?'<label for="v109ChessLine">Example</label> <select id="v109ChessLine">'+d.lines.map((l,i)=>'<option value="'+i+'">'+escape(l.title)+'</option>').join('')+'</select>':'')+'<div class="v109-board" role="group" aria-label="Worked chess line"></div><p id="v109ChessPosition"></p><p id="v109ChessStatus" role="status"></p><div class="v109-controls"><button type="button" data-chess-back>Previous move</button><button type="button" data-chess-next>Show next move</button><button type="button" data-chess-reset>Reset position</button></div>';
 const draw=()=>{const frames=d.lines[line].frames,f=frames[pos],next=frames[pos+1],board=host.querySelector('.v109-board');v109DrawBoard(board,f.pieces,[...(f.marks||[]),...(selected?[selected]:[])],square=>{if(!next){$('v109ChessStatus').textContent='End of this worked line. Use Previous move or Reset to see it again.';return}if(square===next.from){selected=square;draw();$('v109ChessStatus').textContent='Selected '+V109_PIECES[f.pieces[square]][1]+' on '+square+'. Next destination: '+next.to+'.';return}if(selected===next.from&&square===next.to){pos++;selected=null;draw();return}$('v109ChessStatus').textContent='The next move in this example is '+next.from+' to '+next.to+'. Use Show next move to see it.'});$('v109ChessPosition').textContent='Position '+(pos+1)+' of '+frames.length+' · '+f.turn+' to move';$('v109ChessStatus').textContent=f.caption;host.querySelector('[data-chess-back]').disabled=pos===0;host.querySelector('[data-chess-next]').disabled=!next};
 host.querySelector('[data-chess-back]').onclick=()=>{if(pos>0)pos--;selected=null;draw()};host.querySelector('[data-chess-next]').onclick=()=>{if(pos<d.lines[line].frames.length-1)pos++;selected=null;draw()};host.querySelector('[data-chess-reset]').onclick=()=>{pos=0;selected=null;draw()};if($('v109ChessLine'))$('v109ChessLine').onchange=e=>{line=Number(e.target.value);pos=0;selected=null;draw()};draw();
}

A cart already moving would keep that velocity under the stated model."}},"physics-2":{"title":"Choose energy when the path is less useful than the drop","explanation":["Imagine a 2 kg sled. Its loss of gravitational potential energy is mass times g times drop: 2×10×5=100 joules. Kinetic energy is ½mv², so for this sled it is ½×2×v², or just v² in the corresponding units. To have 100 joules of kinetic energy, v² must be 100. Taking the square root gives a speed of 10 m/s.","Try a 4 kg sled on the same ideal hill. It loses 200 joules through the same drop, but its kinetic-energy expression is now 2v². Setting 2v²=200 again gives v²=100. A heavier sled has more energy available and needs more energy for the same speed; that is why mass cancels. The vertical drop matters, not the slope length, because gravitational potential energy changes with height."],"decision":"If friction transfers some energy into heat, do not put the full mgh into kinetic energy. The resistor transfers electrical energy to heat; it does not use up the charge so that less charge comes out than goes in during steady operation.","V=IR is the relationship for the ohmic resistor we are modeling. The stationary cart contributes no initial momentum, but its mass still joins the moving pair afterward.","During the collision each cart pushes on the other. Reactions that break down food supply electrons to a chain of proteins in its inner membrane. As electrons move through that chain, some proteins move hydrogen ions across the membrane. This creates both a concentration difference and an electrical difference between the two sides. That stored difference can drive ions back through ATP synthase, a protein that helps join ADP and phosphate to make ATP.","Oxygen accepts electrons at the end of the chain and participates in forming water. That second picture is where the difference appears. A path that is clear before the move tells us the capture is possible, not whether it wins material for free.","mistake":{"attempt":"The bishop reaches f7 in both positions, so it wins a free knight in both.","diagnosis":"The movement is the same, but the defending king is not. With a king on g8, f7 can be recaptured legally in this sparse position.","repair":"After Bxf7+, show Kxf7. Remove the knight, then remove the bishop. Count the actual pieces exchanged instead of stopping after White’s capture.","check":"Bishops and knights are often valued at about three points each, but those values are rough guides. The specific position still determines whether a trade is useful."}},"chess-5":{"title":"Connect the pawn moves to the piece moves","explanation":["On the starting board, White’s bishop on f1 is shut in by its own pawns. Moving the e-pawn from e2 to e4 opens the diagonal through e2 and also puts a pawn in the center. After Black answers e5, White can develop the knight from g1 to f3. That knight attacks the pawn on e5. Black’s Nc6 both develops a knight and protects that pawn.","White’s Bc4 then uses the diagonal the first pawn move opened: f1–e2–d3–c4. The bishop points toward f7, but that pawn is protected by Black’s king. Looking at a target does not mean taking it would win something. Black can develop with Bc5. These moves put pieces to work; they are not a guarantee of an advantage. If Black plays a different move, inspect what changed instead of forcing the remembered sequence onto a new position."],"decision":"For each move, name a job you can see on the board: occupy the center, open a path, develop a piece, or answer a threat. Then check the opponent’s last move. A familiar move is useful only if it still fits the position.","mistake":{"attempt":"I learned e4, Nf3, Bc4, so I should always play them without looking at Black’s replies.","diagnosis":"Black’s moves can change the pawn structure and create threats. Memorizing only White’s half of an opening misses those changes.","repair":"Compare 1 e4 e5 with 1 e4 d5. In the second line, the pawn on d5 directly attacks e4. One legal continuation is exd5 Qxd5 Nc3, with the knight then attacking the queen.","check":"Describe what the opponent just attacked or opened before choosing your next move. An opening principle helps you think; it does not replace inspecting the board."}}};
let v107NavObserver=null;
function v107Enhance(){
 const lesson=V107_TEACHING[current?.id],first=$('v105Worked'),second=$('v106Second');
 if(!lesson||!first||!second)return;
 const explanation=document.createElement('section');explanation.id='v107Reasoning';explanation.className='v107-teaching';explanation.setAttribute('aria-labelledby','v107ReasoningTitle');
 explanation.innerHTML='<p class="v105-label">Let’s work through the idea</p><h2 id="v107ReasoningTitle">'+escape(lesson.title)+'</h2>'+lesson.explanation.map(p=>'<p>'+escape(p)+'</p>').join('')+'<aside class="v107-decision"><h3>How I choose the approach</h3><p>'+escape(lesson.decision)+'</p></aside>';
 first.before(explanation);
 const correction=document.createElement('section');correction.id='v107Correction';correction.className='v107-teaching v107-correction';correction.setAttribute('aria-labelledby','v107CorrectionTitle');
 const m=lesson.mistake;
 correction.innerHTML='<h2 id="v107CorrectionTitle">Let’s fix a wrong turn</h2><p class="v107-attempt-label">An incorrect attempt</p><blockquote>'+escape(m.attempt)+'</blockquote><h3>Where it goes wrong</h3><p>'+escape(m.diagnosis)+'</p><h3>Work the correction with me</h3><p>'+escape(m.repair)+'</p><h3>Check the reasoning</h3><p>'+escape(m.check)+'</p>';
 second.before(correction);
 const nav=$('v97Main').querySelector('.v101-toc');
 for(const [id,label] of [['v107Reasoning','Work through the idea'],['v107Correction','Fix a wrong turn']]){
  const a=document.createElement('a');a.href='#'+id;a.textContent=label;a.onclick=e=>{e.preventDefault();$(id).scrollIntoView({block:'start'})};nav?.append(a);
 }
 for(const id of ['v101How','v107Reasoning','v105Worked','v107Correction','v106Second','v101Explain','v101Practice']){const a=nav?.querySelector('a[href="#'+id+'"]');if(a)nav.append(a)}
 // Wrapped navigation must not cover the heading it scrolls to on a small screen.
 v107NavObserver?.disconnect();
 if(nav){const adjust=()=>{const offset=Math.ceil(nav.getBoundingClientRect().height)+16;for(const id of ['v101How','v107Reasoning','v105Worked','v107Correction','v106Second','v101Explain','v101Practice']){const section=$(id);if(section)section.style.scrollMarginTop=offset+'px'}};adjust();if(window.ResizeObserver){v107NavObserver=new ResizeObserver(adjust);v107NavObserver.observe(nav)}}
}
